וטעם **אני ה׳‎ אלהיכם אשר הוצאתי אתכם.** בעבור שהזכיר כי גרים הייתם בארץ מצרים על כן אתם חייבין לשמור חקותי ומשפטי בלבבכם ועשיתם אותם:


Scripture states, *I am the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Egypt*, because it earlier mentioned *for ye were strangers in the land of Egypt* (v. 34). Therefore, you are obligated to keep My statutes and ordinances in mind and observe them.



### Verse 37

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 19:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:37:1)


וטעם **אני ה׳‎.** שתרדפו אחרי החקות ששמתי בכם כי כלם צדק:


The meaning of *I am the Lord* is, you shall pursue my statutes which I placed upon you, for they are all righteous.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:1:1)


וטעם **וידבר ה׳‎ אל משה לאמר.** בעבור כי אלה המצות חיוב על כל ישראל ואין גר עמם על כן כתוב בתחלה אל כל עדת ישראל ועתה יחל להזכיר עונש הזנות על כל מי שהוא דר בא״י אזרח או גר תושב והחל מהקשה שבכלם והוא אשר יתן מזרעו למולך והטעם לשכב עם עובדת עבודת כוכבים:


[AND THE LORD SPOKE UNTO MOSES, SAYING.] Scripture earlier¹ stated, *unto all the congregation…of Israel*, because the commandments, which followed, are obligatory upon all of Israel.² Strangers have no part in them.³ Scripture now begins to mention the punishment for harlotry, which will befall anyone who lives in the Land of Israel, be he native born⁴ or resident alien.⁵ It begins with the most severe of them all, namely, with the individual who gives of his seed to Molech, that is, the one who has intercourse with a woman who worships idols.⁶ 4:9: “If a man says *And thou shalt not give any of thy seed to set them apart to Molech* (Lev. 18:21) means, and thou shalt not give of thy seed to make it pass to heathendom, they put him to silence with a rebuke.”

footnotes:
¹ Lev. 19:2.
² That is, only upon Israel.
³ Non-Jews do not share these laws with Israel.
⁴ An Israelite.
⁵ A non-Jew living in Israel. Hence our chapter explicitly notes that what follows applies to both an Israelite and a non-Israelite.
⁶ This interpretation is rejected by the Mishnah. See Megillah



### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:2:1)


וטעם **עם הארץ.** ארץ שהוא דר בה אזרח או גר וזה בפרהסיא:


The meaning of *the people of the land* is, the people of the land wherein the culprit resides, be he a native or a resident stranger.⁷ This⁸ applies to one who publicly offers his offspring to Molach.

footnotes:
⁷ The term “the land” indicates a specific land. Hence I.E.’s comment.
⁸ Stoning.



### Verse 3

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:1)


**ואני אתן את פני באיש ההוא.** אם היה בסתר ויש אומרים כי טעמו להכרית זרעו:


I ALSO WILL SET MY FACE AGAINST THAT MAN. If the sin was committed secretly.⁹ Others say that the reference¹⁰. is to the extirpation of the culprit’s seed.

footnotes:
⁹ For if the sin is committed in public, the culprit is executed (v. 2).
¹⁰ I also will set my face against that man


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:2)


**למען טמא את מקדשי.** שהוא בתוך ארץ ישראל:


TO DEFILE MY SANCTUARY. Which is in the Land of Israel.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:3)


**ולחלל את שם קדשי.** שישמעו הגוים:


AND TO PROFANE MY HOLY NAME. For nations will hear.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ Of the sin that was committed in the Land of Israel.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3:4)


והיה נראה לנו כי טעם באבן שם המין כמו ויהי לי שור וחמור ודברי הקבלה נכונים והם אמת. ויאמר רבי יונה המדקדק הספרדי נ״ע כי פירוש ובמשפחתו הדומה אליו ומי הביאנו בצרה הזאת רק פירוש כמשמעו כי עם הארץ יעלימו עיניהם בעבור שהם ממשפחתו:


It would have appeared to us that *ba-even* (with stones)¹² (v. 2) is a collective noun.¹³ is to be rendered with stones. Compare, *And I have oxen* (shor),¹⁴ *and asses* (chamor)¹⁵ (Gen. 32:6).¹⁶ However, the words of tradition are correct.¹⁷ literally. According to the Midrash the culprit was first stoned by one stone. See *Sanhedrin* 6:4, “The place of stoning was twice the height of a man. One of the witnesses knocked him down…If he died that sufficed; but if not, the second witness took a stone and dropped it on his heart. If he died that sufficed; but if not, he was stoned by all Israel.” They are true. Rabbi Jonah the Spanish grammarian who rests in paradise says that *and against his family* (v. 5) means all those like him.¹⁸ Who brought us into this trouble?¹⁹ Our verse is to be taken literally. The people of the land²⁰ will hide their eyes because they are of his family.²¹

footnotes:
¹² Literally, with a stone.
¹³ Hence ba-even
¹⁴ Literally, an ox.
¹⁵ Literally, an ass.
¹⁶ Literally, and I have an ox and an ass.
¹⁷ The rabbis took ba-even
¹⁸ Rabbi Jonah was bothered by God’s threatening the family of the sinner with punishment when they apparently did not commit any sin.
¹⁹ Of not interpreting our verse literally.
²⁰ Spoken of in verse 4.
²¹ In other words, the family spoken of in our verse is to be identified with the earlier mentioned (v. 4) people of the land who hid their eyes from the sinner



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:5:1)


וטעם **ואת כל הזונים אחריו.** שאם לא יומת יזנו אחרים:


[AND ALL THAT GO ASTRAY AFTER HIM.] This means others will go astray if the culprit is not put to death.



### Verse 6

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:6:1)


**והנפש אשר תפנה אל האבת.** טעמו כאשר אכרית הנותן מזרעו למולך בסתר או בגלוי אם לא ימיתוהו עם הארץ כן אכרית הזונה מאחרי לפנות אל האובות ומצאנו נפשים גם כל נפש ארבעה עשר וכן והכרתי אותו:


AND THE SOUL THAT TURNETH UNTO THE GHOSTS. This means as I will cut off the one who gives from his seed to Molech in secret or in public if the people of the land do not kill him, so will I cut off the one who strays from me and turns to ghosts. We find the term *nefashim* (souls) (Ezek. 20:13) in Scripture.²² (souls) is masculine. *Nefesh* (soul) is usually feminine. However, our verse reads, *ve-hikhratti oto* (and will cut him [the soul] off). It thus treats *nefesh* as masculine. I.E. therefore points out that *nefesh* is sometimes treated as a masculine in Scripture. We also find *all the souls* (nefesh) *were fourteen* (arba’ah asar)²³ (fourteen) is masculine. Thus Gen. 46:22 treats *nefesh* as masculine. (Gen. 46:22). We similarly read,²⁴ *and will cut him*²⁵ (oto) *off*.

footnotes:
²² Nefashim
²³ Arba’ah asar
²⁴ In our verse.
²⁵ The soul.



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:7:1)


**והתקדשתם כי אני ה׳‎ אלהיכם.** קדש ונתתי לכם חקת לשמרם כדי לקדשכם וכבר הזכיר זה הכתוב והטעם להכניס את הגרים התושבים עם ישראל שהם חייבים להיותם קדושים בעבור שידורו בארץ קדושה:


SANCTIFY YOURSELVES THEREFORE, AND BE YE HOLY; FOR I AM THE LORD YOUR GOD. *Ki ani Adonai Elohekhem* (for I am the Lord your God) means for I the Lord your God am holy. I have given you statutes to observe in order to sanctify you.²⁶ Scripture has already mentioned this.²⁷ It repeats this command so as to include the strangers residing among Israel, for they too are obligated to be holy so that they may continue to be allowed to dwell in a holy land.

footnotes:
²⁶ That is, I who am holy have given you statutes in order to sanctify you.
²⁷ See Lev. 11:44.



### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9:1)


והחל להזכיר עונש הכתובים בתחלת הפרשה מהאב כאשר החל איש אמו ואביו תיראו. וטעם להזכיר האב קודם האם בעבור כבודו ואיננו כמו איש אמו ואביו תיראו כי לא יומת אם לא היה בר מצוה:


Scripture begins the list of punishments at the beginning of the section²⁸. with the person who curses his father in the same manner that Scripture earlier starts with *Ye shall fear every man his mother, and his father* (Lev. 19:3).²⁹ (Lev. 19:2). Our verse mentions the father before the mother out of respect for the father. It is unlike *Ye shall fear every man his mother, and his father*,³⁰ for he is not put to death if he has not reached the age of *mitzvah* observance.³¹

footnotes:
²⁸ That is, the list of punishments for the sins following verse 7, which reads, Sanctify yourselves therefore and be holy
²⁹ The preceding verse is, Ye shall be holy; for I the Lord your God am holy
³⁰ Where the mother is placed before the father.
³¹ Hence our verse speaks of an adult. Scripture thus places the father before the mother. However, Lev. 19:3 speaks of a minor. Hence it places mother before father. See I.E.’s comment on Lev. 19:3: “The reason Scripture mentions the mother before the father is that a child first recognizes only its mother. Then it recognizes its father.”


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9:2)


**אשר יקלל את אביו.** מושך עצמו ואחר עמו וכן הוא ואשר יקלל את אמו. וטעם **אביו ואמו קלל.** כאומר תועבה גדולה עשה:


FOR WHATSOEVER MAN THERE BE THAT CURSETH HIS FATHER OR HIS MOTHER SHALL SURELY BE PUT TO DEATH.³² The phrase *asher yekallel* (that curseth) is to be read as if written twice. Our verse is to be read as if written, “and whoever curses his mother.”³³ *He hath cursed his father or his mother* is another way of saying he has committed a great abomination.

footnotes:
³² Literally, For whatsoever man there be that curses his father and his mother shall surely be put to death.
³³ Our verse should be interpreted as if written: For whatsoever man there be that curses his father or that curses his mother shall surely be put to death.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9:3)


**דמיו בו.** כמו דמו בראשו והנסקלים גם הנחנקים דמיהם בם וכלל אומר בכל המיתות כי צריכים אנחנו למסורת אבותינו כי לא נוכל להוציאם להמיתם מהכתוב גם כן לא נכתב בן כמה שנים יהיה בר מצוה


HIS BLOOD SHALL BE UPON HIM. Compare, *his blood shall be upon his head* (Josh. 2:19).³⁴ The blood of those who are stoned and strangled is upon them.³⁵ I will now state a general rule concerning all executions. We cannot derive the manner of execution from Scripture. Hence we need the tradition of our fathers.³⁶ Scripture similarly does not state at what age one is obligated to observe the commandments.³⁷

footnotes:
³⁴ He, not the one who executes him, is responsible for his death.
³⁵ They are responsible for their deaths.
³⁶ If we want to know how the death penalty is applied.
³⁷ And is subject to the death penalty.



### Verse 10

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:10:1)


ואחר כן הזכיר ואיש אשר ינאף את אשת איש כי אסורה היתה: וטעם **אשר ינאף את אשת רעהו.** כמו אביו ואמו קלל כי עשה דבר רע:


[AND THE MAN THAT COMMITTETH ADULTERY WITH ANOTHER MAN’S WIFE.] Scripture next mentions, *and the man that committeth adultery with another man’s wife*,³⁸ for she was prohibited.³⁹ The meaning of *even he that committeth adultery with his neighbor’s wife* is similar to *he hath cursed his father or his mother* (v. 9). It means he committed an evil act.

footnotes:
³⁸ Shall be put to death.
³⁹ Scripture earlier prohibited adultery (Lev. 18:20). However, Lev. 18:20 does not describe the punishment for this sin. Our verse does. The prohibition against adultery is not stated in our verse, for it was stated in Lev. 18:20.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:10:2)


**והנואפת.** אם היתה אנוסה לא תקרא בשם הזה:


AND THE ADULTERESS. Women who are raped would not be so termed.⁴⁰

footnotes:
⁴⁰ A married woman who is raped is not executed, for a raped woman is not an adulteress.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:11:1)


**ערות אביו גלה.** דבר גדול עשה:


HE HATH UNCOVERED HIS FATHER’S NAKEDNESS. He has committed a great sin.



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:12:1)


**כלתו.** אשת בנו:


HIS DAUGHTER-IN-LAW. *Kallato* means his daughter-in-law.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:12:2)


**תבל.** פירשתיו:


CORRUPTION. I have previously explained the meaning of *tevel* (corruption).⁴¹

footnotes:
⁴¹ See I.E. on Lev. 18:23.



### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:13:1)


**תועבה עשו.** אם הנשכב איננו אנוס:


BOTH OF THEM HAVE COMMITTED ABOMINATION. If the one who was penetrated was not raped.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:14:1)


**ואיש אשר יקח את אשה וגו׳‎ זמה הוא.** כטעם זנות במחשבה:


AND IF A MAN TAKE WITH HIS WIFE ALSO HER MOTHER, IT IS WICKEDNESS. *Zimmah* (wickedness) means a promiscuous thought.⁴² comes from the Hebrew root *zayin, mem, mem* which means to plan, devise, purpose. Hence I.E.’s comment.

footnotes:
⁴² Such an act is the result of promiscuous thinking or planning. The word zimmah


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:14:2)


**ואתהן.** זאת או זאת אם היתה האם אשתו תשרף הבת וכן הפך הדבר:


AND THEY. This one or that one. If the mother⁴³ was his wife, then the daughter is burned.⁴⁴ The same is true in the reverse.

footnotes:
⁴³ Of the woman with whom he had intercourse.
⁴⁴ If the daughter of the woman with whom he had intercourse was his wife, then the mother is burned.



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:15:1)


**ואת הבהמה תהרוגו.** שלא תחטיא אחר ויש אומרים להסתיר הקלון:


AND YE SHALL SLAY THE BEAST. So that it not cause another to sin.⁴⁵ Others say, to hide the shame.⁴⁶ 7:4, “Lest the beast should go through the market and people say, this is the beast by reason of which so and so was stoned.”

footnotes:
⁴⁵ If the animal had intercourse with a human being it might let another person approach it for sexual purposes.
⁴⁶ People looking at the animal will be reminded that a human being committed a sexual act with it. See Mishnah, San.



### Verse 16

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:16:1)


**לרבעה.** שם הפעל ואם הוא בחיר״ק כמו בשברי לכם כי משקלי שמות הפועלים הם משתנים:


AND LIE DOWN THERETO. *Le-rivah* (and lie down thereto) is an infinitive even though it is vocalized with a *chirik*,⁴⁷ (and lie down thereto) is vocalized with a *chirik* rather than with a *kamatz katan* (*le-rovah*). Compare, *le-korvah* (Ex. 36:2). for the forms of the infinitive change. Compare, *be-shivri* (when I break) (Lev. 26:26).⁴⁸ is vocalized with a *chirik* rather than with a *kamatz katan* (*be-shovri*).

footnotes:
⁴⁷ Le-rivah
⁴⁸ Be-shivri



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:1)


**ואיש אשר יקח את אחותו.** במקום שאינם יודעים בו ואח״כ נודע הדבר:


AND IF A MAN SHALL TAKE HIS SISTER, HIS FATHER’S DAUGHTER. In a place where he is unknown and afterwards the facts become known.⁴⁹

footnotes:
⁴⁹ A person would not dare to marry a woman whom everyone knows to be his sister.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:2)


וטעם **וראה את ערותה.** או ראה כמו אביו ואמו קלל וראה כמו גלה:


The meaning of *and see*⁵⁰. *her nakedness* is, or see her nakedness.⁵¹ It is like *he hath cursed his father or his mother* (v. 9).⁵² (literally, and his mother). Here the *vav* has the meaning of or. See I.E. on verse 9. See means uncover.

footnotes:
⁵⁰ Hebrew, ve-ra’ah
⁵¹ Engage in intercourse outside of marriage (Krinsky). According to I.E. our verse is to be understood as follows: If a man marries his sister or has sexual intercourse with her outside of marriage.
⁵² Hebrew, ve-et immo


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:3)


וטעם **והיא תראה את ערותו.** שהסכימו שניהם על זה:


The meaning of *and she see his nakedness* is that they both agreed to this.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:4)


**חסד הוא.** מגזרת פן יחסדך שומע וטעמו תוספת בזנות:


IT IS A SHAMEFUL THING. The word *chesed* (shameful thing) is related to *yechassedkha* (revile thee) in *Lest he that heareth revile thee* (Prov. 25:9). *Chesed hu* (it is a shameful thing) means it is an inflated act of harlotry.⁵³ usually has the meaning of an act of kindness, which is beyond the norm. Here it means an especially perverse evil act.

footnotes:
⁵³ Chesed


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:5)


**ערות אחתו גלה.** באונס:


HE HATH UNCOVERED HIS SISTER’S NAKEDNESS. Employing force.⁵⁴

footnotes:
⁵⁴ Hence this additional clause.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:17:6)


**עונו ישא.** לבדו. וטעם להזכיר אלה הדברים באחות בעבור שגדלו יחד והם נערים משחקים וכל עת יתייחד עמה ולא הזכיר הכתוב עונש בת הבן או בת הבת כי סמך על תורה שבעל פה גם יתכן שלא הזכירם בעבור חוסר תאות האב כי הוא בא בימים הזמן שתהיה לו בת בוגרת ועל כן לא הזכיר הכתוב אם האב ואם האם כי הן זקנות:


HE SHALL BEAR HIS INIQUITY. He alone shall bear his iniquity. The reason scripture mentions these things⁵⁵ regarding the sister is that they grew up in one house and are young people who play. The brother is always alone with his sister. Scripture does not mention the punishment for intercourse with the daughter of one’s son or the daughter of one’s daughter, for it relied on the oral Torah.⁵⁶ 51a. It is possible that they are not mentioned because of the father’s lack of desire,⁵⁷ for he is advanced in years when he has a mature daughter. It is for this reason that Scripture does not mention the mother of one’s father and the mother of one’s mother, for they are aged.⁵⁸

footnotes:
⁵⁵ Marriage, consensual intercourse outside of marriage, and rape. It does not mention all three with regard to the other prohibited relations.
⁵⁶ See Sanhedrin
⁵⁷ For his granddaughter.
⁵⁸ Hence their grandsons have no desire to have intercourse with them.



### Verse 18

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:18:1)


**דוה.** היא חולה בעבור הדם:


SICKNESS. She is ill because of her blood.⁵⁹

footnotes:
⁵⁹ She is menstruating.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:18:2)


וטעם **מקרה הערה.** שעשה תועבה גדולה:


*And shall uncover her nakedness* means that he committed a great abomination.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:18:3)


וטעם **והיא גלתה.** ברצונה כי אם היא אנוסה לבדו יכרת:


The meaning of *and she hath uncovered* is that she willingly uncovered, for if she was forced to have intercourse the man alone is cut off.



### Verse 19

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:19:1)


**וערות אחות אמך.** טעמו כבר הזהרתי לא תגלה והנה המגלה את הערוה הערה שארו:


AND…THE NAKEDNESS OF THY MOTHER’S SISTER. Its meaning is, I have previously⁶⁰ warned you not to uncover the nakedness of thy mother’s sister.⁶¹ However, our verse reads, *And…the nakedness of thy mother’s sister…* Why the anomaly? Hence I.E.’s comment. Now the one who uncovers the nakedness⁶² has made naked his near kin.⁶³ is another way of saying he has committed a heinous act. See I.E. on verse 9.

footnotes:
⁶⁰ Lev. 18:12,13.
⁶¹ All the other verses open with, And the man that… And if a man lie… And if a man take…
⁶² Of his mother’s sister or his father’s sister.
⁶³ For he hath made naked his near kin


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:19:2)


**עונם ישאו.** אם הדבר ברצונם לא הזכיר עונש אחות האב גם לא הזכיר כלל עונש שתי אחיות והמשכיל יבין גם דברי הקבלה אמת:


THEY SHALL BEAR THEIR INIQUITY. If they willingly acted thus. Scripture does not mention the punishment for having intercourse with the sister of one’s father. It also does not even hint at the punishment for taking two sisters.⁶⁴. However, there is no mention of the punishment for marrying two sisters. The intelligent will understand why.⁶⁵ The words of tradition are also true.⁶⁶ (excision). See *Sifra* on the Torah portion *Tzav* 13:2.

footnotes:
⁶⁴ Regarding the one who marries his aunt, Scripture writes, they shall hear their iniquity
⁶⁵ For Amram married the sister of his father and Jacob married two sisters (Krinsky; Weiser).
⁶⁶ According to the rabbis they shall be punished with karet



### Verse 20

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:20:1)


**את דדתו.** היא אשת דודו:


WITH HIS UNCLE’S WIFE. *Dodato* refers to his uncle’s wife.⁶⁷

footnotes:
⁶⁷ The wife of his father’s brother. See Lev. 18:14.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:20:2)


**ערירים ימותו.** אמרו הצדוקים שהוא כמו ערומים ופירוש ימותו כמו יומתו ולא אמרו אמת והנכון שהוא כדברי המתרגם ארמית וכבר פירשתי כתבו את האיש הזה ערירי כי הוא כמו הולך ערירי:


THEY SHALL DIE CHILDLESS. The Sadducees⁶⁸ say that *aririm* (childless) means naked and that the meaning of *yamutu* (they shall die) is, they shall be put to death.⁶⁹ They did not speak the truth. The correct interpretation is found in the words of the Aramaic translator,⁷⁰, they shall die childless. for *aririm* is similar to *ariri* (childless) in *I go hence childless* (ariri) (Gen. 15:2).⁷¹ means childless. I have previously explained *Write ye this man childless* (ariri) (Jer. 22:30).⁷²

footnotes:
⁶⁸ This is the opinion of Anan and other Karaites (Krinsky). I.E. often refers to the Karaites by the term Sadducees.
⁶⁹ They shall be executed naked.
⁷⁰ Onkelos renders aririm yamutu
⁷¹ In other words, aririm
⁷² We do not have I.E.’s comment on Jeremiah.



### Verse 21

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:21:1)


ואמר על אשת אח **נדה** והטעם ראוי שירחק ממנה וכן אמרו אחיכ׳‎ שונאיכם מנדיכם והנה אין בעריות זכר נדה כי אם באשת אח והנדה אסורה גם תבוא עת להיות מותרת גם כן יתכן באשת האח כאשר אפרש בפרשת כי ישבו אחים יחדו ואשר אמרו כאשר היא אחות האב אסורה כן אסור לאשה שתנשא לדודה לא זה הדרך כי העריות הזכרים הם המכריחים ואין מי יכריחם ואם בדרך שקול דעתם החסרה יראה לנו למה לא נקבל עדות שתים נשים ואם על הערכים תהיינה ארבעת נשים כמו שני עדים על כן אנחנו צריכים לקבלה:


[IT IS IMPURITY.] Scripture employs the term *niddah* (impurity) with regard to a brother’s wife. The meaning of *niddah hiy* (it is impurity)⁷³, she is distanced. The term *niddah* usually refers to a menstruating women. is, it is fitting for him to distance himself from her.⁷⁴ comes from the root *nun, dalet, dalet*, which means to wander. Similarly, *Your brethren that hate you, that cast you out* (menaddekhem)…*have said* (Is. 66:5).⁷⁵, which is related to the word *niddah*, has the meaning of to distance. Note, there is no mention of *niddah* in all of the prohibited sexual partners⁷⁶. except with regard to a brother’s wife. A *niddah*⁷⁷ is prohibited.⁷⁸ However, a time comes when she is permitted.⁷⁹, and they immerse themselves seven days after the cessation of their menstrual flow, with 12 days being the minimum. This possibility,⁸⁰ as I will explain in the section *If brethren dwell together* (Deut. 25:5), also arises in the case of a brother’s wife. My reply to those who say,⁸¹ as it is prohibited to marry the sister of the father (v. 19) so it is prohibited for a woman to marry her uncle⁸² is: this is not the way of Scripture.⁸³ It is the males who are dominant⁸⁴ (your husband) *shall rule over thee*. with regard to the prohibited sexual practices.⁸⁵ No one dominates the male.⁸⁶ Let those who accept the faulty logic of those who offer the above interpretation⁸⁷ show us why we should not accept the testimony of two women.⁸⁸ If they⁸⁹ argue from the laws of evaluation⁹⁰ then four women should be equal to two male witnesses. We therefore need tradition.

footnotes:
⁷³ I. E. renders niddah hiy
⁷⁴ The word niddah
⁷⁵ The word menaddekhem
⁷⁶ None of the prohibited sexual partners is termed a niddah
⁷⁷ A menstruating woman.
⁷⁸ Verses 18,19.
⁷⁹ Seven days after the start of her menstrual flow. See Lev. 15:19. It should be noted that today all women experiencing a menstrual flow are considered zibbot
⁸⁰ The possibility of permissibility.
⁸¹ The reference is to the Karaites.
⁸² The Karaites believe that it is prohibited for a woman to marry the brother of her father. They argue that there is no difference between a man taking his aunt, which is explicitly prohibited by Scripture (v. 19), and a woman marrying her uncle.
⁸³ Scripture does not legislate prohibitions of choosing sexual partners upon women, for it is the man who does the “taking” (Weiser). Or, the male plays the dominant role in the marriage. Thus it is proper to dominate a niece. However, it is unseemly for a nephew to dominate the sister of his father or his mother (Filwarg).
⁸⁴ See Gen. 3:16, And he
⁸⁵ It is the male who does the taking. If Scripture had wanted to prohibit a man from marrying the daughter of his brother it would have said so.
⁸⁶ No female legally dominates a male.
⁸⁷ That as it is prohibited to marry the sister of the father, so it is prohibited for a woman to marry her uncle.
⁸⁸ If we argue that as males may not marry their aunts so nieces may not marry their uncles, then we are in effect saying that what applies to males also applies to females. Applying this logic we should argue that as males are fit to be witnesses so are females. However, the Karaites like the Rabbinites do not accept the testimony of women.
⁸⁹ The Karaites.
⁹⁰ That women are inferior to men and therefore cannot serve as witnesses. According to Lev. 27:3 a male’s valuation is 50 shekels and a female’s 30.

**ושמרתם את כל חקתי.** שלא לעבור על אלה העריות:


YE SHALL THEREFORE KEEP ALL MY STATUTES. Not to violate the laws regarding these prohibited sexual practices.

[VOMIT YOU OUT.] I will explain this in the Torah portion *Va-Yelech*.⁹¹

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:25:1)

[THE CLEAN.] The ten mentioned beasts.⁹⁵ All the others are unclean.

⁹⁵ The ten clean beasts mentioned in Deut. 14:4,5.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:25:2)

*The unclean fowl* means the unclean fowl which are mentioned.⁹⁶ All others are clean.

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:25:3)


וטעם **לטמא.** שתדעו שהוא טמא במחשבה ובדבור וכן תשקצו מן העוף על כן גר תושב לא יאכל טמא בארץ טהורה כי על זה התנאי ידור בתוכנו:


The meaning of *to hold unclean* is that you shall know in your mind and in your speech⁹⁷ that it is unclean. Similarly, *ye shall have in detestation among the fowls* (Lev. 11:13).⁹⁸ is, you shall know in your mind and in your speech that these fowl are unclean. A resident alien⁹⁹ shall therefore not eat that which is unclean in the clean land. He shall dwell in the land on this condition.¹⁰⁰.

### Verse 26

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:26:1)


ואם תשמרו כל אשר צויתי אתכם אז תהיו קדושים כי חייבים אתם ללכת אחרי כי קדוש אני:


If you keep all that I command you, you will then be holy. You are obligated to follow Me,¹⁰¹ *for I the Lord am holy*.

¹⁰¹ Follow My word. The literal translation is, walk after Me.



### Verse 27

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 20:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:27:1)


וטעם **ואיש או אשה.** בעבור שאמר על האובות לטמאה בהם אמר היו קדושים והמיתו אשר יטמא נפשו באוב ובידעוני כי עד כה לא הזכיר עונש פונה אל אוב בפרהסיא. וטעם להזכיר האשה כי זאת האומנות ביד הנשים יותר וכן מכשפה לא תחיה:


[A MAN ALSO OR A WOMAN.] The reason Scripture states *A man also or a woman that divineth by a ghost or a familiar spirit, shall surely be put to death* is that the Torah earlier had said concerning ghosts and familiar spirits, *seek them not out to be defiled by them* (Lev. 19:31). Scripture now says, be holy and put to death the one who defiles himself with a ghost or a familiar spirit. Previously it did not mention the punishment for publicly turning to a ghost. Scripture mentions *woman*, for this art is mostly practiced by women. Similarly, *Thou shalt not suffer a sorceress to live* (Ex. 22:17).¹⁰²

footnotes:
¹⁰² Scripture mention a sorceress because women practice sorcery more than men. See I.E. on Ex. 22:17 (Vol. 2, p. 487).



## Chapter 21


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:1)


**אמר אל הכהנים.** אחר שהזהיר ישראל ובני אהרן בכללם להיותם קדושים הזהיר בני אהרן שהם חייבים להשמר מדברים אחרים בעבור שהם משרתי השם ויתכן להיות פירוש אמור אל הכהנים כל הפרשה הנזכרת כי התורה בידם:


SPEAK UNTO THE PRIESTS. After charging the Israelites, and the sons of Aaron who are included among them, to be holy,¹ the Lord told the sons of Aaron that they are commanded to keep themselves from other things, for they are the ministers of God. It is possible that *speak unto the priests* refers to the section which is mentioned above,² for the Torah is in the hands of the *kohanim*.³ to teach these laws to the children of Israel. See Deut. 31:9; 17:8-13.

footnotes:
¹ In chapters 18, 19, and 20.
² That is, in the above-noted chapters.
³ It is the duty of the kohanim


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:2)


**ואמרת אליהם.** טעמי המצות שהם חייבים לשמרם לבדם:


AND SAY UNTO THEM. This refers to the commandments which they alone are obligated to keep.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ The laws, which follow.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:3)


**לנפש.** הוא המת:


FOR THE DEAD. *Le-nefesh* (for the dead) refers to the dead.⁵ usually refers to a living body.

footnotes:
⁵ The word nefesh


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:4)


מלת **יטמא.** מבנין התפעל ודגשות הטי״ת להתבלע התי״ו:


The word *yittamma* (defile) is a *hitpa’el*. The *tet*⁶. receives a *dagesh* because the *tav*⁷. is, swallowed.⁸. In other words, the *dagesh* compensates for the missing *tav* of the *hitpae’el* form.

footnotes:
⁶ Of yittamma
⁷ Of the hitpa’el
⁸ By the tet


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1:5)


וטעם **בעמיו.** בכל ישראל שהם עמיו:


The meaning of *be-amav* (among his people) is among all of Israel, who are his people.⁹ (among his people) literally means in his people. Hence I.E.’s comment.

footnotes:
⁹ Be-amav



### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:2:1)


**כי אם לשארו הקרוב אליו.** היה נראה לנו כי פירושו כמו איש איש אל כל שאר בשרו שם כלל ואחר כן פרט לאמו ולאביו:


EXCEPT FOR HIS KIN. It had appeared to us that the meaning of *she’ero* (His kin) is similar to *she’er* (near of kin) in *None of you…to any that is near of kin to him* (Lev. 18:6). In other words, *she’ero* is a general term,¹⁰ with the details *for his mother, and for his father* following.

footnotes:
¹⁰ Meaning a close relative.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:2:2)


וטעם **בעל בעמיו.** שלא יטמא הבעל באשתו וכאשר ראינו שהעתיקו רבותינו כי יטמא לאשתו ושמו לשארו כדרך אסמכתא כאשר פירשתי במלת לעם נכרי ואמרו כי פירוש בעל גדול שהעם ברשותו כמו בעליו אין עמו בטל הפירוש הראשון. וטעם להזכיר אמו קודם האב כי הזכר חי יותר מהנקבה ברוב:


The meaning of *ba’al be-amav* (a chief man among his people) (v. 4) is that a husband¹¹. shall not defile himself for his wife. However, since we saw that our teachers transmitted to us the law that a *kohen* is permitted to defile himself for his wife,¹² 22b. the first interpretation falls away. They employed the word *she’er* as a support,¹³ (his kin) literally means his flesh. According to the rabbis the reference is to a wife. The sages render *ki im le-she’ero* (except for his kin), except for his wife. According to I.E. the plain meaning of *she’ero* is not his wife. He believes that the rabbis used this verse as a support for a law which they knew by tradition. as I explained in my comments on the phrase *unto a foreign people* (Ex. 21:8).¹⁴ means to any stranger. According to I.E. this is not the literal meaning of this phrase. The rabbis merely used this verse as a support. See I.E. on Ex. 21:8 (Vol. 2, pp. 458,459). The rabbis said that the meaning of *ba’al* is, a chief who rules over the people, as in *the owner thereof* (be-alav) *not being with it* (Ex. 22:13).¹⁵ (He shall not defile himself, being a chief man among his people) is: “Seeing that he is fit to become the highest and most honored one among his people [i.e., a high priest], it (Scripture) warns him not to profane his distinction with the impurity of the dead” (Nachmanides, Chavel translation). The reason Scripture mentions the mother of the *kohen* before the father is that in most cases the male lives longer than the female.¹⁶ will mourn for his mother before he does so for his father. Hence she is mentioned first.

¹² Yevamot
¹³ She’ero
¹⁴ According to the rabbis unto a foreign people
¹⁵ According to the rabbis the meaning of lo yittamma ba’al be-amav
¹⁶ In most cases the kohen

**הקרובה אליו.** להיותה אחותו מאב ואם והנה לא יטמא בעל בעמיו בכהנים או בישראלים חוץ מהנזכרים:


THAT IS NEAR UNTO HIM. In that she is his sister from his father and his mother.¹⁷ indicates that the sister spoken of is a full sister. Now, aside from those mentioned,¹⁸ a chief (v. 4) shall not defile himself for any of his people, be they *kohanim* or Israelites.

### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:4:1

**להחלו.** דגשות הלמ״ד להתבלע אחר והוא שם הפעל מבנין נפעל:


TO PROFANE HIMSELF. The *lamed*¹⁹. has a *dagesh* in it to compensate for the missing other *lamed*.²⁰ is spelled with one *lamed*. However, it comes from a root that has two *lameds*. The *dagesh* in *hechallo* compensates for the missing root letter. *Le-hechallo* (to profane himself) is an infinitive in the *nifal*.

THE CORNERS OF THEIR BEARD. Over the dead, as is the practice in places in the land of the Chaldeans. The meaning of the *corners of thy beard* (Lev. 19:27) is thus clarified.²¹

וטעם **שרטת.** אפילו אחת וכבר נזהרו ישראל על אלה. וטעם הזהירם כי ראש מוקרח וזקן מגולח ובשר שרוט לא ישמש לפני השם:


*Saratet* (cuttings) means even one cut.²² (cuttings) is singular. Hence I.E’s. comment. Israel has already been cautioned not to do these things.²³ The reason Scripture warns the *kohanim*²⁴ about this is that one who has a bald head, or whose beard has been shorn, or whose flesh is cut shall not serve before the Lord.

### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:7:1)


**אשה זונה.** לא מצאנו על דרך הפשט בכל המקרא זונה כי אם כמשמעה גם כן ויתנו את הילד בזונה כאשר פירשתיו:


WOMAN THAT IS A HARLOT. If we follow the plain meaning of the text then we do not find in all of Scripture the word *zonah* (harlot) to have any but its literal meaning.²⁶ is a woman who sells her body. According to the rabbis a *zonah* is a woman who had intercourse with an individual whom she is not permitted to marry, i.e., a woman who has relations with a close relative or with a non-Jew. Such a woman is considered a *zonah* even if the intercourse was not with her consent. Hence I.E.’s comment. The meaning of and *have given a boy for a harlot* (zonah) (Joel 4:3) is also as I have explained.²⁷ in Joel 4:3 refers to a harlot. It means that they gave a young man in payment for sexual favors.

²⁶ That is, a zonah
²⁷ In other words, zonah


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:7:2)


**וחללה.** שאינה מפורסמת כזונה ולפי הפשט היה נראה מדברי יחזקאל כי אלמנות כהן מותרות לכהנים לכן דברי הקבלה הוא הנכון:


OR PROFANED. A woman who is not known as a harlot.²⁸. It is also possible that I.E. refers to a woman who is promiscuous but not a prostitute. It should be noted that according to the rabbis the reference is to a woman born of a marriage between a *kohen* and a woman prohibited to him (*Kiddushin* 77a). According to the plain meaning, it would appear from the words of Ezekiel²⁹ that the widow of a *kohen*³⁰. is permitted to marry other *kohanim*.³¹ may marry is the widow of another *kohen*, for Ezekiel states, *Neither shall they take for their wives a widow…but they shall take…a widow that is the widow of a priest*. The words of tradition³² may marry any widow. See *Kiddushin* 78b. are thus true.³³ from marrying any widow. We thus must interpret Ezek. 44:22 as follows: Neither shall a *kohen gadol* take for their wives a widow…but some of the *kohanim*, that is, a common *kohen*, may take a widow (any widow, not only the widow of a *kohen*). See *Kiddushin* 78b.

### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:9:1)


**כי תחל.** לדעת רבים מגזרת תחלה ולפי דעתי מלשון חלול רק הוא מהבנין הכבד הנוסף כמו לא יחל דברו ועל שני הפירושים המלה היא מפעלי הכפל רק היא זרה ויתכן שתהיה זרה החל גם תחל שלא יתערבו עם טעם תחלה בעבור היות התי״ו קמוץ בקמץ קטן וכן לבלתי החל:


IF SHE PROFANE HERSELF BY PLAYING THE HARLOT. Many believe that *techel* (profane) is related to *techillah* (beginning).³⁸ (if she profane herself by playing the harlot) as if she begins to play the harlot. However, I believe that it is related to the word *chillul* (profane) but it is a *hifil*. Compare, *he shall not break* (yachel)³⁹ *his word* (Num. 30:3). According to both interpretations the word comes from a double root.⁴⁰. However, it is irregular.⁴¹ rather than with a *kamatz* as similar roots are in the *hifil*. Compare, *achel* (I will begin), *tachel* (she will begin), etc. It is possible that the word *hechel* (profaned) (Ezek. 20:9) and the word *techel* are both irregular in that the *tav*⁴² of *techel* is vocalized with a *tzereh*⁴³. so that it not is confused in meaning with *techillah* (beginning).⁴⁴. If both were similarly vocalized, we would confuse them. Similarly, *hechel* (profaned)⁴⁵ is vocalized with a *tzere* rather than with a *kamatz* so that it not be confused with *hachel* (begin) (Deut. 2:24). in *that it should not be profaned* (Ezek. 20:9).

⁴¹ For its prefix is vocalized with a tzere
⁴² The tav
⁴³ Literally, a small kamatz
⁴⁴ Beginning and profane both come from the same root, chet, lamed, lamed
⁴⁵ Hechel

### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:11:1

NEITHER SHALL HE GO IN TO ANY DEAD BODY. *Nafshot met* is to be read as if written *nafshot guf met* (dead body). **ומן המקדש לא יצא.** אמרו המעתיקים אחר המת והוא הנכון ויש אומרים בשבעת ימי המלואים וזה לא יתכן כי כבר באר ומלא את ידו גם כן יתכן שלא יצא מן המקדש כי אם לדבר מצוה:


NEITHER SHALL HE GO OUT OF THE SANCTUARY. After the dead, so said the transmitters of tradition.⁵⁵ 18a. Also see Rashi. What they said is correct. Some say that the reference is to the seven days of consecration. However, this cannot be so, for Scripture has already stated, *and* [the kohen] *that is consecrated* (v. 10).⁵⁶ who is already consecrated. It is also possible that the meaning of our verse is that the *kohen gadol* shall not go out of the sanctuary except for the purpose of observing a precept.

### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:13:1)


**בבתוליה.** יש שמות בלשון הקדש לא יפרדו לעולם כמו נעורים וזקונים ועלומים ובתולים ויש שמות בלשון הקדש שלא יתחברו נטף ושכם וזהב וברזל:


IN HER VIRGINITY. There are nouns in Hebrew that never come in the singular. Compare, *ne’urim* (youth), *zekunim* (old age), *alumim* (youth), and *betulim* (virginity). There are other words which never come in the plural, i.e., *taf* (children), *shekhem* (shoulder), *zahav* (gold), and *barzel* (iron).

The reason Scripture reads *but a virgin*⁶⁰ may only marry a virgin was stated in the previous verse. Why repeat it again in our verse? is that it wants to add *of his own people*, for a captive virgin⁶¹ and a convert⁶² are prohibited to him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:15:1)


**ולא יחלל זרעו.** פירוש אלמנה וגרושה בסתר כי את כל אלה לא יקח בגלוי ואחר שהזכיר קדושת הכהנים החל להזכיר המומין:


AND HE SHALL NOT PROFANE HIS SEED. By taking a widow or a divorced woman in secret. *These shall he not take* (v. 14) means he shall not publicly take any of these women.⁶³ After Scripture mentions the sanctity of the *kohanim* it begins to list the blemishes.⁶⁴ from offering sacrifices.

footnotes:
⁶³ Since Scripture has already stated that he may not publicly take a divorced woman, a widow, a harlot, or a profaned woman. Hence our verse refers to secret intercourse with these women.
⁶⁴ Which disqualify a kohen



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:17:1)


**לחם אלהיו.** הוא קרבן המזבח ואלה הכהנים הדיוטים על כן אמר כל מי שהוא מזרע אהרן הכהן:


THE BREAD OF HIS GOD. The reference is to the sacrifices offered on the altar. The *kohanim* spoken of in our verse refer to common priests. Scripture therefore states anyone who is of the seed of Aaron the *kohen*.



### Verse 18

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:18:1)


**חרום.** הפך שרוע והנה חרום מגזרת חרם:


MAIMED. *Charum* (maimed) is the reverse of *saru’a* (too long). Note, *charum* is related to the word *cherem* (destruction).⁶⁵ refers to a person who has an extra-large limb while *charum* refers to a person who has a limb that is undersized.

footnotes:
⁶⁵ According to I.E. saru’a


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:18:2)


**שרוע.** מגזרת מהשתרע:


TOO LONG. *Saru’a* (too long) is related to the word *me-histare’a* (to stretch himself) (Is. 29:20).



### Verse 20

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:1)


**או גבן.** קרוב מגזרת הרים גבנונים והנו״ן שרש והוא שם התאר:


OR CROOK-BACKED. The word *gibben* (crook-backed) is most probably related to the word *gavnunnim* (peaks) in *ye mountains of peaks* (Ps. 68:17).⁶⁶ is someone who is extremely tall. The *nun*⁶⁷. is a root letter.⁶⁸ (crook-backed) to *gavnunnim* (peaks). *Gibben* is an adjective. Similarly the word *dak* (a dwarf) is to be interpreted according to the plain meaning.⁶⁹

footnotes:
⁶⁶ According to I.E. gibben
⁶⁷ Of gibben
⁶⁸ Hence we can relate gibben
⁶⁹ Of the verse.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:2)


כן **דק** והוא כמשמעו להיות דק קצר קומה:


*Dak* means short of stature.⁷⁰ stands in contrast to *gibben*. If *gibben* means a peak, then *dak* is its opposite.

footnotes:
⁷⁰ Dak


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:3)


**תבלול בעינו.** יש אומרים מגזרת תבל עשו השחתה ויש אומרים מגזרת בלולה בשמן והתי״ו על שני הפירושים נוסף:


HATH HIS EYE OVERSPREAD. Some say that *tevallul* (overspread) is related to the word *tevel* (corruption) in *they have wrought corruption* (Lev. 20:12). *Tevallul* means a corruption.⁷¹ Others say that *tevallul* is related to the word *belullah* (mingled) in *mingled with oil* (Lev. 2:5).⁷²). According to both interpretations the *tav* of *tevallul* is superfluous.⁷³ is *bet, lamed, lamed*.

footnotes:
⁷¹ A blemish.
⁷² A streak of white going into the iris or pupil. See Rashi and Ibn Janach (Sefer Ha-Rikmah
⁷³ For the root of tevallul


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:4)


גם **גרב** בעין על דעת רבים כן:


According to the opinion of many the scab (*garav*) is also in the eye.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:5)


**ילפת.** כטעם דבק מגזרת וילפת שמשון ויש אומרים עוות מגזרת ויחרד האיש וילפת והיו״ד נוסף כיו״ד יצהר:


OR SCURVY. *Yalefet* (scurvy) means sticking.⁷⁴ It is related to the word *va-yilpot* (took fast hold) in *And Samson took fast hold* (Jud. 16:29). There are others who say that *yalefet* means crooked.⁷⁵ with a crooked hand, foot, fingers, etc. It is related to the word *va-yillafet* (turned) in *that the man was startled, and turned* (Ruth 3:8). The *yod* of *yalefet* is superfluous.⁷⁶. It is like the *yod* of *yitzhar* (oil) (Num. 18:12).⁷⁷.

footnotes:
⁷⁴ It refers to boils which stick to the body.
⁷⁵ A kohen
⁷⁶ For its root is lamed, peh, tav
⁷⁷ The root of which is tzadi, heh, resh


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:6)


**מרוח.** שם התאר מגזרת רוח:


CRUSHED. *Mero’ach* (crushed) is an adjective. It is related to the word *ru’ach* (wind).⁷⁸

footnotes:
⁷⁸ See Nachmanides: “One who has wind [and a result of which] his testicles are swollen” (Chavel translation).


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20:7)


**אשך.** הביצה ואחר שפרט אמר כלל כל איש אשר בו מום:


STONES. *Ashekh* (stones) refers to the testicle. After listing specific blemishes the Torah issues a general statement and says, *no man…that hath a blemish* (v. 21).



### Verse 21

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:21:1)


**וטעם מום בו.** כמו אביו ואמו קלל:


The meaning of *hath a blemish* is similar to *he hath cursed his father or his mother* (Lev. 20:9).⁷⁹

footnotes:
⁷⁹ The repetition is for emphasis. See I.E. on Lev. 20:9.



### Verse 22

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:22:1)


**לחם אלהיו.** לחם הפנים והאשם והחטאת שהם קדשי הקדשים כנגד השלמים כי גם השלמים קדש:


THE BREAD OF HIS GOD…OF THE MOST HOLY. The showbread, the guilt offering, and the sin offering, which in comparison to the peace offerings are most holy, for the peace offerings are also holy.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:22:2)


**ומן הקדשים.** המעשר והבכור:


AND OF THE HOLY. The tithe and the first-born.



### Verse 23

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:23:1)


**אך אל הפרכת לא יבא.** להיותו כהן גדול גם אל המזבח לא יקרב:


ONLY HE SHALL NOT GO IN UNTO THE VEIL. Although he is a *kohen gadol*.⁸⁰ is struck with a blemish he is prohibited from going unto the veil. A common *kohen* may not go unto the veil. Hence I.E.’s comment. He shall also not approach the altar.

footnotes:
⁸⁰ If the kohen gadol



### Verse 24

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 21:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:24:1)


וטעם **ואל כל בני ישראל.** שלא יזבח להם שלמים בעלי מום לולי הקבלה ובעבור שהזכיר כי בעל מום יאכל קדש הזהיר שיהיה האוכל טהור:


[AND UNTO ALL THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL.] If it were not for the tradition⁸¹ who are blemished to slaughter peace offerings for the children of Israel we would interpret *and unto all the children of Israel* as meaning that those who are blemished should not slaughter peace offerings for the children of Israel. Now, since Scripture mentions that a blemished *kohen* may eat that which is holy, it warns⁸² that the one who eats sacred food must be clean.

footnotes:
⁸¹ Which permits kohanim
⁸² In the section which follows.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2:1)


**וינזרו.** כמו והזרתם והוא מבנין נפעל והטעם ירחקו ויבדלו מגזרת נזיר:


THAT THEY SEPARATE THEMSELVES. *Ve-yinnazeru* (that they separate themselves) is similar to *ve-hizzhartem* (thus shall ye separate) (Lev. 15:31).¹. *Ve-yinnazeru* is a *nifal*. It means they shall distance and separate themselves. It is related to the word *nazir* (nazirite).² separates himself from wine and uncleanliness.

footnotes:
¹ Both words come from the root nun, zayin, resh
² A nazir


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2:2)


**ולא יחללו.** מושך עצמו ואחר עמו וכן הוא ולא יחללו אתם שם קדשי ולא יחללו אשר הם מקדישים לי:


AND THAT THEY PROFANE NOT. *And that they profane not* applies to what immediately follows it and to what follows later. Our verse is to be read as follows: And that they profane not My holy name, and that they profane not that which they hallow unto Me.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2:3)


וטעם **אני ה׳‎.** קדוש:


The meaning of *I am the Lord* is, I the Lord am holy.



### Verse 3

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:3:1)


וטעם **מלפני אני ה׳‎** כי אחר שיכרת מלפני השם לא יעמוד כאשר פירשתי בסוד השם:


[FROM BEFORE ME: I AM THE LORD.] This means since he is cut off from God, he will cease to exist.³ I have explained the latter in my comments on the secret of God’s name.⁴ indicates that God alone exists forever and the existence of all things is contingent upon Him. See I.E. on Ex. 3:15 (Vol. 2, p. 67).

footnotes:
³ God is the cause and sustainer of existence. To be cut off from God thus means to be cut off from the source of life; i.e., his soul following his death ceases exist.
⁴ The name YHVH



### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:4:1)


**עד אשר יטהר.** ימי טהרתו:


UNTIL HE BE CLEAN. Until the days necessary for his cleansing have passed.



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:5:1)


**אשר יטמא לו.** בשבילו כמו אמרי לי אחי הוא כל אלה הנזכרים יטמאו עד הערב:


WHEREBY HE MAY BE MADE UNCLEAN. The word *lo* means whereby.⁵ literally means to him, or to it. Hence I.E.’s comment. Compare the word *li* (of me)⁶ literally means to me. in *say of me*: *He is my brother* (Gen. 20:13). All those mentioned⁷ shall become unclean until evening.

footnotes:
⁵ Lo
⁶ Li
⁷ In our verse.



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:7:1)


**ובא השמש וטהר.** ידוע כי וטהר שב אל הטמא כמו וכפר עליה הכהן וטהרה כי אין בפסוק זכר יום רק חז״ל העתיקו אפילו שיבא השמש לא יאכל עד סור האור ושמו זה הפסוק לאסמכתא כאשר פירשתי בפסוק לעם נכרי על כן אמרו טהר יומא:


AND WHEN THE SUN IS DOWN, HE SHALL BE CLEAN. It is known that *he shall be clean* refers to the unclean, for there is no mention of the word day in the verse.⁸ (and he shall be clean) cannot refer to the day. Compare, *and the priest shall make atonement for her, and she shall be clean* (Lev. 12:8). However, the rabbis of blessed memory transmitted to us the law that even if the sun sets he shall not eat until it is completely dark.⁹ (and he shall be clean) refers to the day. They render *ve-taher* as, and it shall be clean; i.e., the day shall be cleared away. See *Berakhot* 2a,b. They used this verse as a support.¹⁰ I have explained the latter¹¹ in my comments on the verse *unto a foreign people* (Ex. 21:8).¹² The rabbis therefore¹³ said that *ve-taher* (he should be clean) means that the day shall be clean.

footnotes:
⁸ Hence ve-taher
⁹ According to the rabbis ve-taher
¹⁰ The Rabbinic interpretation is not the real meaning of the verse.
¹¹ The Rabbinic use of a verse as a support for their traditions.
¹² See Vol. 2, p. 458.
¹³ Because they used this verse as a support.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:7:2)


**כי לחמו.** מאכלו וכבר פירשתיו:


BECAUSE IT IS HIS BREAD. His food.¹⁴ means his food. I have previously explained this.¹⁵ (bread) means food. See I.E. on Ex 16:4 (Vol. 2, p. 316).

footnotes:
¹⁴ His bread
¹⁵ That lechem



### Verse 8

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:8:1)


וריקי מוח יפרשו הפסוק שהוא בספר יחזקאל והוא כל נבלה וטרפה מן העוף ומן הבהמה לא יאכלו הכהנים כל מה שנבלוהו וטרפוהו עופות או בהמות וזה הבל כי הנה בתורה כתוב **כל נבלה וטרפה לא יאכל** והטעם שאם יאכל לא ישמש:


Those whose brains are empty explain the verse in Ezekiel, which reads, *The priests shall not eat of any thing that dieth of itself, or is torn, whether it be fowl or beast* (Ezek. 44:31), as meaning that the priests shall not eat anything brought down or torn by animals or fowl.¹⁶). Deut. 44:21 prohibits an Israelite from eating meat which dies of itself (*nevelah*). Ezek 44:21 seems to limit the prohibition to eat *nevalah* and *terefah* only to *kohanim*. In order to harmonize Ezekiel and the Torah, there were those who claimed that Ezekiel spoke of an animal that has been properly slaughtered and then ripped apart and befouled by birds and beasts. In other words, the Torah prohibits any Israelite from eating the meat of any animal that has been ripped apart by an animal of prey, while Ezekiel adds that a *kohen* is also prohibited from eating kosher meat that has been befouled. It should be noted that according to this interpretation the word *nevelah* is not to be taken literally but means flesh which is as disgusting as meat that dies of itself (Filwarg). For other interpretations see Sarim and Meijler. However, this is nonsense, for the Torah states all that dies of itself or is torn he¹⁷. shall not eat.¹⁸ The meaning¹⁹ is if he eats²⁰ he shall not minister.²¹ eats that which was torn or died of itself he is ritually unclean and shall not serve in the tabernacle or eat sacred food.

footnotes:
¹⁶ Ex. 22:30 prohibits an Israelite from eating that which is torn (terefah
¹⁷ The kohen
¹⁸ I.E.’s paraphrase of verse 8, which literally reads: that which dies of itself, or is torn, he shall not eat.
¹⁹ Of verse 8 and Ezek. 44:21.
²⁰ That which dies of itself or is torn.
²¹ The meaning of our verse and that of Ezekiel is, if a kohen



### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:9:1)


**ושמרו את משמרתי.** רמז למקדש על כן ולא ישאו עליו חטא ומתו בו:


THEY SHALL THEREFORE KEEP MY CHARGE. *Mishmarti* (my charge) alludes to the sanctuary. Scripture therefore reads, *lest they bear sin for it* (alav), *and die therein* (vo).

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:10:1)


**וכל זר.** שאינו מזרע אהרן:


THERE SHALL NO COMMON MAN. One who is not of the seed of Aaron.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:11:1)


**הם יאכלו בלחמו.** זכרים ונקבות:


THEY MAY EAT OF HIS BREAD. Males and females.



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:12:1)


**בתרומת הקדשים.** השוק והחזה:


THAT WHICH IS SET APART FROM THE HOLY THINGS. The thigh and the breast (Lev. 10:15).



### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:13:1)


**מלחם אביה תאכל.** כמו בראשונה:


SHE MAY EAT OF HER FATHER’S BREAD. As at first.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:13:2)


**וכל זר לא יאכל בו.** על בן אם יש לה ובעבורו לא תאכל היא:


BUT THERE SHALL NO COMMON MAN EAT OF IT. The reference is to a son, if she has one. She cannot eat of the holy food because of her son.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:14:1)


**ונתן לכהן את הקדש.** טעמו עם הקדש או תהיה מלת ונתן לכהן מושכת עצמה ואחרת עמה וכן פירושו ויסף חמשיתו עליו ונתן לכהן ונתן את הקדש ולפי דעתי שאין צורך בעבור מלת עליו:


AND SHALL GIVE UNTO THE PRIEST THE HOLY THING. The meaning of *ve’et ha-kodesh* (the holy thing) is, with²² here has the meaning of with. the holy thing.²³ implies that only the holy thing is to be given to the *kohen*. The question thus arises, what is to be done with the fifth part? Hence this interpretation. On the other hand, *and shall give unto the priest* might apply to what precedes it and to what follows it. Our verse is to be interpreted as follows: Then he shall put the fifth part thereof unto it and shall give it unto the priest, and he shall give unto the priest the holy thing. I believe that there is no need for this interpretation, for Scripture reads *unto it*.²⁴ takes in the fifth part.

footnotes:
²² In other words, et
²³ According to I.E. our clause is to be interpreted as follows: And shall give the fifth part thereof along with the holy thing. And shall give unto the priest the holy thing
²⁴ Unto the holy thing. Thus, and shall give unto the priest the holy thing



### Verse 16

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:16:1)


**והשיאו אותם.** פעל יוצא לשנים פעולים והטעם שיזהירום ויורום עד שלא ישגו:


AND SO CAUSE THEM TO BEAR. *Ve-hisi’u* (and cause…to bear) is a *hifil*. Its meaning is, they shall caution them and teach them so that they do not err.²⁵ do not teach the Israelites they cause them as it were, to bear the iniquity of profaning the holy food.

²⁵ If the kohanim



### Verse 18

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:18:1

ואחרי שהזכיר קדשי בני ישראל הזהיר שלא יביאו הקדשים מבעלי המומין:


After mentioning the holy things of the children of Israel²⁶ Scripture goes on²⁷ to caution the Israelites to not bring the holy things from blemished animals.

²⁶ The offerings of the children of Israel. See verse 2.

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:18:2)


וטעם **ומן הגר.** כי משפט אחד לנודר ולמתנדב לישראל ולגר כי כן כתוב וחכמים הפרישו בין נדר ובין נדבה וכל נדר נדבה ואין כל נדבה נדר וכל קרבן שיעלה כלו על המזבח ראוי להיותו בלא מום::


[STRANGERS.] Scripture reads *or of the strangers* for it is stated that there is one law for the Israelite and the stranger who offer a vow or freewill offering.²⁸ The sages differentiated between a vow offering and a freewill offering.²⁹ 1:1. All vow offerings are freewill offerings. However, not all freewill offerings are vow offerings.³⁰ It is fitting that all sacrifices that are offered in their entirety³¹ on the altar be unblemished.³²

CLEARLY UTTERED. *Le-fale* means to clearly utter.



### Verse 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:22:1)


**עורת.** שם התאר לעין:


BLIND. *Avveret* (blind) is an adjective relating to the eye.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:22:2)


וי״א כי **שבור** ביד וחרוץ ברגל וחרוץ מגזרת כן משפטך אתה חרצת כמו גזור:


Some say that *shavur* (broken) means a break in the hand and *charutz* (maimed) refers to an injury in the foot. *Charutz* is related to *charatzta* (hast decided it) in *So shall thy judgment be*; *thyself hast decided it* (I Kings 20:40). *Charutz* means cut.³³

footnotes:
³³ The term “cut” can also be applied to decree or decision. This is its meaning in I Kings 20:40.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:22:3

**יבלת.** כמו תבלול וכללו של דבר אנחנו נסמוך על הקבלה ולא נשען על דעתנו החסרה:


A WEN. *Yabbelet* (wen) is similar to *tevallul* (overspread) (Lev. 21:20). The general rule is: we rely on tradition,³⁴ not on our deficient minds.

**לא תקריבו אלה לה׳‎.** לפלא נדר ולא יקח מאלה האמורים:


YE SHALL NOT OFFER THESE UNTO THE LORD. In fulfillment of a clearly uttered vow. Nor shall the *kohen* take the organs from these.³⁵.

³⁵ And make an offering by fire of them



### Verse 23

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:23:1)


**שרוע.** כראשון: **וקלוט** הפכו והוא מגזרת עיר מקלט:


TOO LONG. *Saru’a* (too long) has the same meaning as when first mentioned (Lev. 21:18).³⁶ TOO SHORT. *Kalut* (too short) is the opposite of *saru’a* (too long). *Kalut* is related to the word *miklat* (refuge) in *the city of refuge* (Josh. 21:13).³⁷ means compressed or held back. A city of refuge limits or holds the refugee back in that he may not leave.

**ומעוך.** מגזרת מעכו שדיהן:


BRUISED. *Ma’uch* (bruised) is related to *mo’akhu* (pressed) in *their bosoms pressed* (Ezek. 23:3).


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:24:2)

OR CRUSHED. *Katut* (crushed) is related to *va-ekkot* (beat) in *and beat it in pieces* (Deut. 9:21). Crushed and bruised refer to wounds in the testicles.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:24:3)

OR TORN. *Natuk* (torn) is related to *yinnatek* (broken) in *as a string of tow is broken* (Jud. 16:9).

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:25:1

FROM THE HAND OF A FOREIGNER. One should not think as follows: I will not hesitate to offer this sacrifice, for it is the offering of a foreigner.³⁹

³⁹ Hence one need not be so strict,


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:25:2)


**משחתם.** המ״ם נוסף והתי״ו שרש מגזרת השחתה ומ״ם להיות להם משחתם שרש והתי״ו נוסף:


THEIR CORRUPTION. The *mem* of *moshchatam* (their corruption) is not a root letter. However, its *tav* is a root letter. *Moshchatam* is related to the word *hashchatah* (destruction).⁴⁰. On the other hand, the *mem* of *moshchatam* (their anointing) (Ex.40:15) is a root letter and the *tav* is not.⁴¹.

⁴⁰ It comes from the root shin, chet, tav

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:25:3)


**ירצו.** מבנין נפעל ואחר שהזכיר לא ירצו הזכיר כי כל קרבן שיקרב קודם יום השמיני גם הוא לא ירצה:


BE ACCEPTED. *Yeratzu* (be accepted) is a *nifal*. After stating that a blemished sacrifice will not be accepted, Scripture goes on to say that any sacrifice brought from an animal less than eight days old will also not be accepted.



### Verse 27

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:27:1)


**שור או כשב או עז.** נקראים על שם סופם כמו ובגדי ערומים תפשיט. יומת המת כי יפול הנופל או הטעם זכר המין:


WHEN A BULLOCK, OR A SHEEP, OR A GOAT. The newborn animals are called by what they ultimately become. Compare, *And stripped the naked*⁴² *of their clothing* (Job 22:6); “the dead⁴³ shall be put to death”⁴⁴ (Deut. 17:6); “for the one who falls shall fall from it”⁴⁵ (Deut. 22:8). On the other hand, Scripture may have been referring to the name of the species.⁴⁶ means when an animal that belongs to the species of bullock, sheep, or goat is born.

⁴⁵ Translated literally
⁴⁶ When a bullock, or a sheep, or a goat is brought forth

AND WHETHER IT BE COW⁴⁹ (ox). OR EWE.⁵⁰ (male sheep). I have previously explained this.⁵¹ and *seh* are the names of the species. In other words, they do not apply only to males. The commandment pertains to both males and females.⁵²

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:29:1)


וטעם **וכי תזבחו זבח תודה.** שיאכל ביום אחד בעבור שהזכיר שור או שה שלא ישחט ביום אחד והוסיף על הפרשה הכתוב בצו את אהרן מלת לרצונכם:


[AND WHEN YE SACRIFICE A SACRIFICE OF THANKSGIVING.] The reason that *And when ye sacrifice a sacrifice of thanksgiving…On the same day it shall he eaten* follows is that Scripture earlier noted it is prohibited to kill a cow or a ewe [and its young together] in one day.⁵³ Scripture adds to what is written in the Torah portion *Tzav* (Lev. 6:2),⁵⁴ *that ye may be accepted*.⁵⁵

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:30:1)


וטעם **אני ה׳‎.** להיות התודה שלימה:


[I AM THE LORD.] Scripture reads *I am the Lord* to teach that the thanksgiving offering is to be “whole.”⁵⁶.

AND YE SHALL KEEP MY COMMANDMENTS. In your hearts.

### Verse 32

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:32:1)


וטעם **ולא תחללו את שם קדשי.** עם בני אהרן ידבר כי הפרשה דבקה והם המצווים שלא ישחטו להם או לישראל אם ובן ביום אחד גם יתכן שמצוה וכי תזבחו זבח תודה לכהנים והעד שהחל בפרשה אחר כן דבר אל בני ישראל ועד שני ונקדשתי בתוך בני ישראל:


[AND YE SHALL NOT PROFANE MY HOLY NAME.] This is directed to the sons of Aaron, for this section⁵⁷ is connected to what is earlier stated.⁵⁸ They⁵⁹ are commanded not to sacrifice for themselves or for Israel a cow and its offspring on one day. It is also possible that *And when ye sacrifice a sacrifice of thanksgiving* (v. 29) is directed to the *kohanim*.⁶⁰. The fact that the following section opens with *Speak unto the children of Israel* (Lev. 23:2) is proof of this.⁶¹. *But I will be hallowed among the children of Israel* is a second witness.⁶². Had it been directed to the Israelites, Scripture would have read: But I will be hallowed among you.

⁶⁰ For the entire section (verses 26-33) is directed to the kohanim
⁶¹ That our chapter (verses 26-33) is directed to the kohanim
⁶² This is directed to the kohanim



### Verse 33

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 22:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:33:1)


**המוציא אתכם** ובסוף אני ה׳‎ שהוא הדבור הראשון והוא עיקר כל המצות ואחר שהזכיר קדשי בני ישראל הזכיר הימים שיקריבו בהם העולות והחל מהשבת:


THAT BROUGHT YOU OUT. The verse ends with *I am the Lord*. This the first statement of the Decalogue⁶³ and the root of all the commandments.⁶⁴ After mentioning the holy things of the children of Israel,⁶⁵ the Torah notes⁶⁶ the days that Israel shall offer burnt offerings. It starts with the Sabbath.

וטעם **אלה הם מועדי.** בעבור היות שבתות רבות בשנה:


[EVEN THESE ARE MY APPOINTED SEASONS.] Scripture reads, *even these are My appointed seasons* (mo’adai) because there are many Sabbaths in the year.¹ refers to all the Sabbaths (of the year. After stating that all the Sabbaths are God’s appointed seasons, Scripture goes on to speak of the weekly Sabbath in verse 3. For another interpretation see Rashi.
