### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:9:1)


**והעברת שופר תרועה.** יש מחלוקת אם נברא העולם בניסן או בתשרי ואין צורך להאריך כי מעתיקי הדת תקנו לנו בתפלות ראש השנה זה היום תחלת מעשיך זכרון ליום ראשון והנה ראינו תקיעת שופר בשנת היובל בתשרי בתחלת השנה ועוד בפרשת הקהל את העם לקרוא בתורה בחג הסוכות וכתוב למען ישמעו ולמען ילמדו ולא יתכן להיות אחר חצי השנה (ועוד וחג האסיף בצאת השנה וכן תקופת השנה) (במ״ט אינו) והעד הנאמן שנת השבת שכתוב לא תזרעו ובמרחשון יחלו בארץ ישראל לזרוע ואם היתה תחלת השנה מניסן הנה לא יקצרו אשר זרעו בשנה הששית ולא יזרעו כי הנה שנת השמטה היא ואחר שלא יקצרו זרע שנה ששית לא יזרעו פעמים והכתוב אמר לא תזרעו בשנה השביעית לבדה ויהודה הפרסי אמר כי ישראל היו מונים בחשבון השמש ואילו היה זה נכון הנה לא פירש משה מהלך שנה תמימה כי חכמי המזלות לא יכלו עד הנה להוציאה לאור כי חכמי הודו מוסיפים על רביע היום חומש שעה ותלמי וחביריו אומרים כי יחסר חלק משלש מאות ביום והוא קרוב ממהלך העבור והבאים אחריו אמרו חלק ממאה ושש ואחרי׳‎ מאה ועשר ואחרי׳‎ מאה ושלשים גם מאה ושמונים כי יש מי שהיא שנתו להשלמת המזלות מנקודה נראית ויש מנקודת הגלגל הנטוי לימין ולשמאל ואנחנו צריכים לקבלה ועוד כי פירוש חדש יכחיש הפרסי והצדוקים אומרים שהדת על שנת הלבנה. דע כי אין ללבנה שנה כלל רק בקשו המחשבים מספר חדשים קרובים לשנת החמה ומצאום י״ב כאשר אין לחמה חדש והמחשבים בקשו מספר לחדש שיהיה נחלק קרוב לחדש מימות חדשי הלבנה על כי חדשינו הם ללבנה ושנותינו ישובו בסוף לשנות החמה על כן העתיקו חז״ל כי לעולם היה בית דין עושה שבעה עבורים בכל מחזור הלכה למשה מסיני אף על פי שהיו קובעים בכל חדש על פי ראיית הלבנה וסוד המחזור ידוע מחכמת המזלות:


THEN SHALT THOU MAKE PROCLAMATION WITH THE BLAST OF THE HORN. There is a difference of opinion as to whether the world was created in Nisan or in Tishri.²¹ 10a, 11a. There is no need to dwell on this, for the transmitters of the law ordained that we are to say in the Rosh Ha-Shanah prayer which they established for us, “This day marks the beginning of Your works, it is a memorial of the first day.”²² We also read in Scripture that the *shofar* is to be sounded in the Jubilee year in the month of Tishri at the beginning of the year.²³ Furthermore,²⁴ places it in parentheses. the Torah, in the portion commanding the people to gather to hear the Torah read states that the Torah is to be read on the festival of Sukkot.²⁵ Scripture writes, *that they may hear, and that they may learn* (Deut. 31:12).²⁶ means that they may hear, and that they may learn the entire year. See I.E. on Deut. 31:12 (Vol. 5, p. 225). It is not possible for this²⁷ to take place after half a year has passed.²⁸ Furthermore,²⁹ edition of I.E. The *Mikra’ot Gedolot* places it in brackets. Scripture states, *and the feast of ingathering, at the end of the year* (Ex. 23:16). Similarly, *and the feast of the ingathering at the turn of the year* (Ex. 34:22).³⁰ The faithful proof is the Sabbatical year, regarding which it is written, *ye shall not sow* (v. 11). They start sowing in the month of Marcheshvan in the Land of Israel. If the beginning of the year started in Nisan, then they would not harvest that which they sowed in the sixth year³¹ and they would not sow,³² for it is the Sabbatical year. Since they will not harvest³³ what they planted in the sixth year, they will not sow twice.³⁴ However, Scripture states that they should not sow in the seventh year alone.³⁵ Judah Ha-Parsi³⁶ said that Israel employed a solar calendar.³⁷ Now if this were so [then we do not know the exact time that a year begins] for, note, Moses did not explain the length of a complete year and so far the astronomers are unable to bring the length of a solar year to light. For example, the wise men of India add a fifth of an hour to the quarter of the day.³⁸ Ptolemy and his friends say that 1/300 of the day is missing.³⁹ This is close to that of the intercalated calendar. Those who came after him said one part of 106.⁴⁰ Others said 110.⁴¹ Still others said 130.⁴² There are those who say 180.⁴³ For there are those who calculate the year when the sun concludes a cycle around the constellations from a visible point.⁴⁴ Others calculate it from a point on the sphere which inclines to the right and the left.⁴⁵ We need tradition.⁴⁶ Furthermore, the meaning of the word *chodesh* (month) contradicts the Persian.⁴⁷ literally means new moon. I.E. will soon show that a solar calendar has no true months, for the new moons play no role in establishing a solar year. The Sadducees⁴⁸ say that the Jewish religion is based on a lunar year. Note, there is no year at all to the moon.⁴⁹ However, as the sun has no months, the calculators⁵⁰ sought⁵¹ a number of months close to that of a solar year.⁵² They found them to be 12.⁵³ The calculators⁵⁴ sought a number of days for the month⁵⁵ that would be close to the number of days into which a lunar month is divided. Our months are therefore lunar months and our years return⁵⁶ at the end of a solar year.⁵⁷ Our sages therefore transmitted to us a law given to Moses from Sinai that the Bet Din always made seven leap years in every cycle⁵⁸ even though they used to establish every month by sighting the moon.⁵⁹ The secret of the cycle⁶⁰ is known from the science of the stars.⁶¹

Now the study of the Torah on the Sabbath starts with the onset of the Sabbath. It does not start at midday. Similarly, the study of the Torah in the Sabbatical year. Furthermore, why waste half of a year that could be devoted to Torah study.

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:9:2)


וטעם **תעבירו.** שיתקעו שופר בכל המזלות:


The meaning of *shall ye make proclamation* is that they should sound the *shofar* in all the roads.⁶². Some emend to *gevulot* (borders) (Meijler). Others to *chuztot* (streets). Some texts read *mazalot* (constellations). This is obviously wrong.

### Verse 10

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:10:1)


**וקדשתם את שנת החמשים.** בעבודת הארץ וכל יושביה:


AND YE SHALL HALLOW THE FIFTIETH YEAR. All the inhabitants with regard to working the land.⁶³ in our context means abstaining from working the land.

footnotes:
⁶³ Hallowing


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:10:2)


**דרור.** ידועה שהוא כמו חפשי וכדרור לעוף עוף קטן מנגן כשהוא ברשותו ואם הוא ברשות אדם לא יאכל עד שימות:


LIBERTY. The meaning of the word *deror* is known. It means liberty. *As the flying swallow* (deror) (Prov. 26:2) refers to a small bird that produces music when it is free.⁶⁴ However, if it is taken captive it refuses to eat and starves to death.

footnotes:
⁶⁴ I.E. renders Prov. 26:2 as the flying free bird.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:10:3)


**יובל.** כמו שלוח וחז״ל אמרו שפירוש יובל כבש והראיה שופרות היובלים ונקראה השנה בשם השופר:


JUBILEE. The word *yovel* (Jubilee) means sent.⁶⁵ The sages, of blessed memory, said that the meaning of *yovel* is a lamb. *Shoferot ha-yovelim* (rams’ horns) (Josh. 6:4) is proof of the latter. The year⁶⁶ is called by the name of the *shofar*.⁶⁷

footnotes:
⁶⁵ The Jubilee is the year when people are set free.
⁶⁶ Of Jubilee.
⁶⁷ The year is called “the ram’s horn,” i.e., the year in which the ram’s horn is sounded.

וטעם **תהיה לכם.** לישראל לבדם:


[BE UNTO YOU.] To the Israelites alone.

**ושבתם איש אל אחזתו.** כאשר יפרש כי בשנת היובל תשוב הארץ הנמכרת לבעליה:


AND YE SHALL RETURN EVERY MAN UNTO HIS POSSESSION. As Scripture goes on to explain,⁶⁸ in the year of Jubilee the land that is sold shall return to its owners.⁶⁹ means that each person shall regain the land that he sold.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:12:2)


**מן השדה תאכלו.** מה שיוציא השדה מעצמו תאכלו כלכם ככתוב בשנת השמטה:


YE SHALL EAT THE INCREASE THEREOF OUT OF THE FIELD. You shall eat what the land produces of itself. It is the same as is written regarding the Sabbatical year.⁷²

### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:13:1)

IN THIS YEAR OF JUBILEE. Its meaning is in the beginning of the year.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:14:1)


**או קנה.** שם הפעל ויחסר מקום קניתם קנה וכן זכור את יום השבת ורבים כמוהם:


OR BUY. *Kanoh* (buy) is an infinitive.⁷³ A word is missing.⁷⁴ is employed as an auxiliary verb. *Kanoh* is in place of *kenitem kano* (you will surely buy).⁷⁵ is short for *kenitem kano*. Compare, *zakhor* (remember) in *Remember the sabbath day* (Ex. 20:8).⁷⁶ is an infinitive. It is short for *zakhor tizkor*. There are many such instances.⁷⁷ The word *timkeru* (thou sell) is in the plural because it shall be sold in front of witnesses.⁷⁸ The same is true of *al tonu* (ye shall not wrong).⁷⁹ is in the plural. It is so because it applies to the buyer or seller and to the witnesses. One is not permitted to be a witness to a transaction in which the buyer or seller is cheated.

footnotes:
⁷³ Employed as an auxiliary verb. I.E. comments thus because an infinitive by itself is out of place here.
⁷⁴ For, as noted, kanoh
⁷⁵ Kanoh
⁷⁶ Zakhor
⁷⁷ Where Scripture employs an infinitive and leaves out the major verb.
⁷⁸ The witnesses are part and parcel of the act of selling. Hence the use of the plural.
⁷⁹ Cheat. Tonu

וטעם **תמכרו.** לשון רבים על פי עדים. וכן טעם **אל תונו.** רק כמספר שנים:


[ACCORDING TO THE NUMBER OF YEARS.] Only according to the number of years.

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:17:1)


וטעם **אל תונו איש את עמיתו.** אזהרה למוכר כי הראשון לקונה:


[AND YE SHALL NOT⁸⁰. The Torah reads *ve-lo*. If not a scribal error, then I.E. quoted from memory and erred. WRONG ONE ANOTHER.] This is to be understood as a warning to the seller,⁸¹. for the first injunction⁸² (v. 14). is directed to the buyer.⁸³ in verse 14 follows *or buy of thy neighbor*.

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:17:2)


**כי אני ה׳‎ אלהיכם.** ואחר שאני אלהיכם אפרע משניהם מאשר יונה עמיתו. ואם תעשו חקותי תתן הארץ פריה וטעם להזכיר זה בעבור מספר תבואות:


FOR I AM THE LORD YOUR GOD. As I am your God, I will exact punishment from the two of them,⁸⁴ from the one who wrongs his neighbor. If you observe My statutes the earth will bring forth its produce. Scripture mentions this⁸⁵. because it earlier spoke of the number of crops⁸⁶ because it earlier said *according unto* the *number of years of the crops he shall sell*. (v. 15). In other words, the thrust of our verse is fear God and do not cheat in calculating the years. (v. 16).

⁸⁴ From the buyer or from the seller.
⁸⁵ For I am the Lord
⁸⁶ Scripture states in our verse, but thou shalt fear thy God; For I am the Lord



### Verse 20

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:20:1)


אמרו הצדוקים כי **תבואתנו** ראיה כי תחלת השנה מניסן ואין זו ראיה כי יתכן להיות פירוש תבואתנו מה שתוציא הארץ מעצמה ואם אמרו לא תקרא תבואה כי אם ספיח הנה לנגדם מן השדה תאכלו את תבואתה ועוד יודיענו איך יעשו בשנת היובל כי לא יזרעו בששית ובשביעית ובשמינית רק יזרעו בתשיעית ויקצרו בעשירית ולמה לא הזכיר הכתוב זה כי אמר לשלש השנים לבדם והנכון בעיני שפירוש ועשת את התבואה שאתן ברכה בששית שיספיק ויוסיף עוד שנה ובשנת היובל יהיו שלש שנים בלא תוספות:


[OUR INCREASE.] The Sadducees⁸⁷ say that *our increase*⁸⁸. proves that the year begins in Nisan.⁸⁹. This is no proof, for it is possible that the meaning of *our increase* is what the earth produces of itself.⁹⁰ If they⁹¹ say that what the earth produces of itself is not called *tevu’ah* (increase)⁹² but *safi’ach*,⁹³ then *ye shall eat the increase thereof* (tevu’atah) *out of the field* (v. 12) contradicts them.⁹⁴). Furthermore, let them inform us how the Israelites were to act in the Jubilee year, for they would not sow in the sixth, seventh,⁹⁵ and eighth year.⁹⁶ They would only sow in the ninth year and harvest in the tenth. Why didn’t Scripture mention this, for Scripture only mentions *for the three years* (v. 21).⁹⁷ It appears to me that the meaning of *and it shall bring forth produce* (v. 21) is, I will place a blessing on the sixth year⁹⁸ that it will be sufficient for the sixth and an additional year.⁹⁹ However, in the Jubilee year there will be food for three years and no more.¹⁰⁰. I.E. interprets the aforementioned as: then I will command My blessing upon you in the sixth year. Now in all Sabbatical years the land will produce food for the sixth and seventh years. However, the sixth year shall bring forth produce for precisely three years when it precedes the year of Jubilee. I.E. comments thus because our verse seems to imply that God will bless the sixth year only when it precedes the Jubilee.

footnotes:
⁸⁷ The Karaites.
⁸⁸ Hebrew, tevu’atenu
⁸⁹ For what is planted in the fall is harvested in the spring. However, if the year begins in Tishri the land is not sown and there is nothing to harvest in the spring. However, our verse indicates that this is not so, for it states, nor gather in our increase
⁹⁰ That which grows of itself.
⁹¹ The Karaites.
⁹² The term used in our verse.
⁹³ See verses 5 and 11.
⁹⁴ For the previous verse speaks of what grows of itself (safi’ach
⁹⁵ In the sixth and seventh years of the seventh cycle of Sabbatical years. They wouldn’t sow in the sixth year because they couldn’t harvest in the seventh year. Sowing was prohibited in the seventh year.
⁹⁶ The eighth year from year one of the seventh cycle of Sabbatical years, that is, the year of Jubilee.
⁹⁷ Verse 21 says that the sixth year will produce food for three years. However, according to the Karaites, Scripture should have said that the land would produce food for four years, i.e., for the sixth, seventh, eighth and ninth years. It should be noted that I.E. interprets the three years in verse 21 as referring to the seventh, eighth and ninth years.
⁹⁸ Of every Sabbatical year.
⁹⁹ The seventh year.
¹⁰⁰ Verse 21 reads, then I will command My blessing upon you in the sixth year, and it shall bring forth produce for the three years



### Verse 21

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:21:1)


ומלת **ועשת** זרה כמו משרת את המלך כי בעבור התחברות שני תוי״ן חסרו האחד להקל על הלשון:


[AND IT SHALL BRING FORTH.] The word *ve-asat* (and it shall bring forth) is irregular.¹⁰¹ is short for *ve-asetat*, which is a variation of *ve-asetah*. See Krinsky and Filwarg. Compare, *ministered* (mesharat) *unto the king* (I Rings 1:15).¹⁰². Scripture reads thus because it drops one of the *tavs* to simplify enunciation when two *tavs* are joined.

footnotes:
¹⁰¹ Ve-asat
¹⁰² The usual form is mesharetet



### Verse 22

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:22:1)


**ישן.** שב על מן וטעמו כעצם וכן מן הכפרת תעשו את הכרובים:


THE OLD STORE. The word *yashan* (the old store) adverts to the word *min* (of).¹⁰³ (produce), which precedes it, for *tevu’ah* is feminine and *yashan* (old store) is masculine. I.E. says that *min ha-tevu’ah yashan* (of the produce, the old store) is to be interpreted as if written *min yashan ha-tevu’ah* (of the very old store of the grain). The meaning of *min* (of) is, of the very thing. Similarly, *with* (min) *the ark-cover*¹⁰⁴ (min) *the ark-cover shall ye make the cherubim* as, of the very ark-cover shall ye make the cherubim *shall ye make the cherubim* (Ex. 25:19).

footnotes:
¹⁰³ Not to the word tevu’ah
¹⁰⁴ I.E renders With



### Verse 23

**לצמיתות.** כמו כריתות והתי״ו שרש מגזרת יצמיתם ה׳‎ אלהינו:


IN PERPETUITY. The word *tzemitut* (perpetuity) is similar¹⁰⁵ to *keritut* (divorcement) (Is. 50:1). The *tav*¹⁰⁶. Its root is *tzadi, mem, tav*. is a root letter. *Tzemitut* is related to the word *yatzmitem*¹⁰⁷. (will cut them off) in *The Lord will cut them off* (Ps. 94:23).¹⁰⁸

### Verse 25

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:25:1)

BE WAXEN POOR. *Yamukh* (be waxen poor) comes from a root which consists of two stem letters that are sounded and one silent letter placed between them.¹¹¹, a verb whose middle root letter is a *vav*. Its root is *mem, vav, kaf*. Its meaning is poor and destitute.¹¹² is to become poor or to become destitute

### Verse 28

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:28:1)


**ויצא.** הממכר. והנה שם תחת תאר השם ורבים כמוהו ואחר שהזכיר בית מושב ידענו כי ומכר מאחזתו שדה או כרם:


IT SHALL GO OUT.¹¹⁴. The latter can be rendered he shall go out, or it shall go out. Hence I.E.’s comment. That which is sold shall go out. Note, a noun¹¹⁵. is used in place of an adjective.¹¹⁶ (that which is sold). Our verse reads *ve-hayah mimkaro…* which literally means, then his sale (*mimkar*) shall remain in the hand of him that has bought it. This makes no sense. Hence I.E.’s comment that it should be interpreted as, then that which he hath sold shall remain in the hand of him that hath bought it. There are many such instances.¹¹⁷ Since Scripture states *a dwelling-house in a walled city* (v. 29),¹¹⁸ (v. 29) cannot be redeemed after a year has passed, then the verses that speak of property always being redeemed and if not redeemed, returning to its owners in the year of Jubilee, must speak of land. we know that *and sell some of his possession* (v. 25) refers to a field or a vineyard.

footnotes:
¹¹⁴ Hebrew, ve-yatza
¹¹⁵ Mimkaro
¹¹⁶ Nimkar
¹¹⁷ Where Scripture employs a noun in place of an adjective.
¹¹⁸ Since Scripture states that a dwelling-house in a walled city



### Verse 29

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:29:1)


**עיר חומה.** עיר מוקפת:


IN A WALLED CITY. A city surrounded by a wall.¹¹⁹ (a dwelling-house in a walled city). *Bet moshav ir chomah* literally means a dwelling-house in a city wall. Hence I.E.’s comment.

footnotes:
¹¹⁹ Our verse reads bet moshav ir chomah


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:29:2)


**ימים.** שנה עד שוב הימים מקור וחום וקיץ וחורף כאשר היו כי סבתם שוב השמש למקומה והנה אנחנו צריכים לקבלה על שנה תמימה אם לחמה אם ללבנה ואם מעוברת:


A FULL YEAR. The word *yamim*¹²⁰ means a year. *Yamim* means until the days return to their point of departure after completing their cycle of cold and hot, summer and winter, for the return of the sun to its place¹²¹ is the cause of this cycle.¹²² Now, we need tradition as to what determines a full year,¹²³ whether it is a solar year, or a lunar year, or an intercalated year.¹²⁴ 9:3, “Rabbi says: He is allowed a year and its intercalary month (i.e., the extra days by which the solar year exceeds the lunar year).”

footnotes:
¹²⁰ Literally, days.
¹²¹ The movement of the sun to its place of departure.
¹²² A year means a full cycle of the sun. The question arises, Why does the Torah refer to a year as days? The answer is, the cycle of the sun is responsible for the full complement of days. Hence a full cycle of the sun can be referred to as days.
¹²³ Spoken of in our verse.
¹²⁴ If the year in which the house is sold contains 13 lunar months, can the house be redeemed in the 13th month or do we consider it to be a year after 12 months have passed? See Arakhin

### Verse 33

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:33:1)


ובעבור שאמר גאלת עולם אמר ואשר יגאל והטעם כמו יקנה ויש אומרים אפילו אם היה הגואל לוי. וי״ו **ויצא** כפ״א רפה בלשון ישמעאל. בית או עיר המשפט אחד:


Scripture reads, *va-asher yigal* (and if a man purchase)¹³² literally means to redeem. (v. 33), because our verse reads *ge’ulat olam*.¹³³ and *ge’ulat* come from the same root. In other words, Scripture employs the word to mean to purchase for poetic reasons. The meaning of *yigal* is shall purchase. There are those who say *va-asher yigal* means even if the redeemer is a Levite.¹³⁴ (and if a man purchase of the Levites) means, and if a man of the Levites purchases. In other words, if a Levite buys a house from another Levite, even in a walled city, that house goes out in the Jubilee. One might think that the law governing a purchase of a house in a Levitical city by one Levite from another is the same as that governing the sale of a house in a walled city by one Israelite from another Israelite, i.e., that the house can only be redeemed during the first year of its purchase. The *vav* of *ve-yatza* (then…shall go out) (v. 33) has the same meaning as the unaspirated *fa* in Arabic.¹³⁵ is not to be rendered as “and” but as “then” or “the like.” For the meaning of the Arabic *fa* see Vol. 1, p. xvi; p. 30. A house and a city (v. 33) have one law.¹³⁶ (the house…in the city), which literally means a house and a city, is to be rendered a house or a city. Both the house or the city sold by a Levite reverts to the Levite seller in the Jubilee year. See *Eruvin* 59a, which speaks of a city owned by an individual.

¹³⁵ The vav
¹³⁶ In other words, bayit ve-ir

### Verse 36

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:36:1)


**נשך ותרבית.** מפורש בדברי הקבלה. תי״ו תרבית נוסף כמו תרמית והוא מגזרת רב רק הוא מהפעלים שהלמ״ד שלהם נח נעלם כמו תכלית שנאה ואחר כן פי׳‎ הנשך והתרבית גם מ״ם מרבית נוסף כמו מ״ם ואבן משכית:


INTEREST…OR INCREASE. These terms¹⁴⁰ and *tarbit*. are explained in the words of tradition. The *tav* of *tarbit* (increase) is superfluous.¹⁴¹ It is like the *tav* of *tarmit* (deceitful) (Zeph. 3:13).¹⁴² *Tarbit* is related¹⁴³ to the word *rav* (many).¹⁴⁴. However, it is a verb that comes from a root whose last letter is silent and hidden.¹⁴⁵. Its root is *resh, bet, heh*. It is similar¹⁴⁶ to the word *takhlit* (utmost)¹⁴⁷. in *utmost hatred* (Ps. 139:22). Scripture next¹⁴⁸ explains the terms *neshekh* (increase) and *tarbit* (increase). The *mem* of *marbit* (increase) (v. 37) is also superfluous. It is like the *mem* of *maskit* (figured)¹⁴⁹. in *any figured stone* (Lev. 26:1).

¹⁴² Which is a form letter and does not have any grammatical meaning.
¹⁴³ In meaning.
¹⁴⁴ From the root resh, bet, bet
¹⁴⁵ A heh
¹⁴⁶ In form.
¹⁴⁷ From the root kaf, lamed, heh
¹⁴⁸ After stating that one is not to take interest and increase (v. 36), Scripture goes on to explain these terms (v. 37).
¹⁴⁹ From the root sin, kaf, heh



### Verse 38

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:38:1)


וטעם **אני ה׳‎ אלהיכם אשר הוצאתי אתכם מארץ מצרים.** שהייתם גרים והנה נתתי לכם אחוזה והטעם דבק עם גר ותושב וחי עמך גם עם כל הפרשה הנזכרת בעבור גאלת הארץ. והזכיר פרשת ומטה ידו עמך בעבור שיזכיר הישראלי שיהיה לו צורך רב עד שימכור עצמו שיצא ביובל והנה כל פרשת הסדר דבקה:


[I AM THE LORD YOUR GOD, WHO BROUGHT YOU FORTH OUT OF THE LAND OF EGYPT.] Where you were strangers. Note, I gave you an inheritance. Our verse is connected in meaning to *as a stranger and a settler shall he live with thee* (v. 35).¹⁵⁰ where you were strangers, and I gave you an inheritance. It is also connected to the already mentioned section,¹⁵¹ for it¹⁵² deals with the redemption of the land. Scripture mentions the section containing *and his means fail with thee* (v. 35) because it will go on to state that an Israelite who is in such great need that he sells himself¹⁵³ shall go out in the year of Jubilee.¹⁵⁴ All the sections of the Torah portion are thus connected.

footnotes:
¹⁵⁰ Our verse, as it were says help the individual who falls, be he an Israelite or a stranger because I am the Lord your God, who brought you forth out of the land of Egypt
¹⁵¹ The section which precedes verses 36 and 37, which deal with the redemption of the land.
¹⁵² The section, which precedes our section.
¹⁵³ To be a slave.
¹⁵⁴ See verses 47-55.



### Verse 39

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:39:1)


**ונמכר לך.** הוא מעצמו או גנב ממך ומכרוהו בבית דין:


AND SELL HIMSELF UNTO THEE. He sells himself to you or he stole from you and he was sold by the Bet Din.



### Verse 41

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:41:1)


**הוא ובניו עמו.** זה שהעתיקו חכמים ועבדו לעולם:


HE AND HIS CHILDREN WITH HIM. The transmitters of tradition based themselves on this verse when they explained the meaning of *and he shall serve him for ever* (Ex. 21:6).¹⁵⁵ means until the Jubilee year. See I.E. on Ex. 21:6 (Vol. 2, p. 458): “*And he shall serve him for ever* means, and he will serve him until the year of Jubilee, for there is no season of Israel that is longer than it.”

footnotes:
¹⁵⁵ According to the rabbis, and he shall serve him for ever



### Verse 42

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:42:1)


וטעם **כי עבדי הם.** כי אני קניתים מבית עבדים:


[FOR THEY ARE MY SERVANTS.] Because I bought them from the house of bondage.



### Verse 43

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:43:1)


**תרדה בו.** כטעם ממשלה:


THOU SHALT NOT RULE OVER HIM. *Tirdeh* means you shall rule.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:43:2)


**בפרך.** כדברי המתרגם ארמית:


WITH RIGOR. *Be-farech* (with rigor) is to be explained as the Aramaic translator does.¹⁵⁶ (with hard work, i.e., backbreaking work).

footnotes:
¹⁵⁶ Onkelos renders be-farech, be-kashyu



### Verse 44

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:44:1)


**מאת הגוים אשר סביבותיכם.** כעמון ומואב ואדום וארם:


OF THE NATIONS THAT ARE ROUND ABOUT YOU. Such as Amon, Moab, Edom, and Aram.



### Verse 45

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:45:1)


**וגם מבני התושבים.** הדרים בארץ כנען שהיא ארצכם והם מהגוים הנזכרים או מצרים וכל עם חוץ משבעת הגוים כי הכתוב הזהיר עליהם לא תחיה כל נשמה והנה אסור אפילו להאכילם ויתכן שחז״ל אמרו עבד כנעני על הגר הגר בארץ כנען ואיננו כנעני בייחוסו או הם ידעו להוציא זה הדבר לאמתו כי דעתינו נקלה כנגד דעתם:


MOREOVER OF THE CHILDREN OF THE STRANGERS THAT DO SOJOURN AMONG YOU. Who dwell in the land of Canaan, which is your country, and they are from the nations that are mentioned.¹⁵⁷ Or the reference is to Egypt and every nation aside from the seven nations,¹⁵⁸ for Scripture commanded regarding the aforementioned, *thou shalt save alive nothing that breatheth* (Deut. 20:16). Note, it is prohibited to even feed them.¹⁵⁹. It is possible that when the sages, of blessed memory, spoke of a Canaanite slave¹⁶⁰ they spoke of someone who lived in Canaan, not of a person of Canaanite ancestry. On the other hand, it is possible that the sages were able to ascertain the truth of the matter,¹⁶¹ 67b. Also see Rashi. for our mind is insignificant when measured against their minds.¹⁶²

footnotes:
¹⁵⁷ In I.E.’s comments on verse 44. I.E. comments thus because the Torah does not permit the Israelites to allow Canaanites to dwell in the Land of Israel.
¹⁵⁸ The seven Canaanite nations.
¹⁵⁹ I.E.’s interpretation of thou shalt save alive nothing that breatheth
¹⁶⁰ Scripture prohibits Canaanites from living in the Land of Israel. However, it appears from the Talmud that Israelites kept Canaanite slaves. This contradicts Biblical law. Hence I.E.’s comments.
¹⁶¹ According to the rabbis the prohibition against keeping a Canaanite slave applies only to the offspring of a Canaanite father. It does not apply to the offspring of a female Canaanite and a non-Canaanite male. Such people were permitted to live in the Land of Israel and they provided the Canaanite slaves. See Kiddushin
¹⁶² In other words, when it comes to Jewish law we accept Rabbinic tradition.



### Verse 46

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46:1)


**והתנחלתם.** מבנין התפעל וכמוהו והתאויתם לכם:


AND YE MAY MAKE THEM AN INHERTANCE. *Ve-hitnachaltem* (and ye may make them an inheritance) is a *hitpa’el*. Similarly, *ve-hitavvitem* (and ye shall mark out a line) in and *ye shall mark out your line* (Num. 34:10).


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46:2)


**לעולם בהם תעבודו.** מותרים רק כאשר מצאנו חז״ל שאמרו כי היא מצוה קבלנוה:


OF THEM MAY YE TAKE YOUR BONDMEN FOR EVER. One is permitted to do so. However, since we find that the sages say that it is a commandment to do so, we accept what they say.¹⁶³ 38b; *Sotah* 3b.

footnotes:
¹⁶³ Actually the sages Rabbi Akiba and Rabbi Yishma’el differ on this point. See Gitin


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46:3)


וטעם **ובאחיכם בני ישראל.** שתעשה הפרש בין אחיך ובין הנכרי:


[BUT OVER YOUR BRETHREN THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL.] Its meaning is, you will make a difference between your brother and the stranger.



### Verse 47

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:47:1)


**או לעקר.** כמו שרש והטעם ששב לדת ישראל והוא ממשפחת גר ואין ריע לו רק הוא כמו מלת ושרשך הפך וכן ואת סוסיהם תעקד:


OR TO THE OFFSHOOT. *Eker* (offshoot) means a root. The reference is to a person who accepted the Jewish religion. He himself is from the family of a stranger. The word *eker* has no neighbor.¹⁶⁴ However, it is similar in meaning to *ve-shereshkha* (and root thee out) (Ps. 52:7). It means the opposite of its stem.¹⁶⁵ means a root. However, *ve-shereshkha* means, and uproots you. Similarly, *eker* comes from the Hebrew stem meaning a root. However, its meaning is someone who has been uprooted, i.e., a stranger. Similarly, *te’akker* (thou shalt hough) in *thou shalt hough their horses* (Josh 11:6).¹⁶⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶⁴ The word is not found again in Scripture.
¹⁶⁵ Shoresh
¹⁶⁶ I.E. renders this clause, you shall uproot their horses.



### Verse 49

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:49:1)


**או השיגה ידו.** שמצא אבדה או ירש ממון מת ממשפחתו. והזכיר בתחלה האח והמשפחה כי הוא הדבר הנמצא ברוב:


OR IF HE BE WAXEN RICH. He found a lost object or he inherited the money of a deceased member of his family. Scripture first mentions the brother and the family¹⁶⁷ because that is what is most usually the case.¹⁶⁸

footnotes:
¹⁶⁷ With regard to the redemption of the slave.
¹⁶⁸ It is more usual for a family member to redeem a slave than for the slave to redeem himself.



### Verse 50

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:50:1)


**קנהו.** בה״א ובלא ה״א שוה:


HIM THAT BOUGHT HIM. *Konehu* (him that bought him) has the same meaning whether spelled with a *heh* or not.¹⁶⁹ has the same meaning as *kono*. The latter is the form most used.

footnotes:
¹⁶⁹ Konehu


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:50:2)


**המכרו.** שם הפעל מבנין נפעל והשנים שיעמוד עמו כימי שכיר יהיה:


HE SOLD HIMSELF. *Himakhero* (he sold himself) is an infinitive¹⁷⁰ in the *nifal*. The years that he was with his master shall be like the days of a hired man.¹⁷¹

footnotes:
¹⁷⁰ Plus the pronominal suffix.
¹⁷¹ In calculating the redemption money, the years served by the slave shall be deducted from the amount of money paid for the slave. The value placed upon the years that the slave served shall be assessed in accordance with what a hired man is paid.



### Verse 51

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:51:1)


וי״ו **ואם** מעט נשאר בשנים. כפ״א רפה בל׳‎ ישמעאל והטעם בין היות השנים הנשארות רבות או מועטות:


[IF THERE BE YET MANY YEARS.] The *vav* in *If there be yet many years…and if* (ve-im) *there remain but few years* (v. 52) is like an unaspirated *fa* in Arabic.¹⁷² in *ve-im* (v. 52) does not mean and. It means or, for verse 52 is not a continuation of verse 51. However, there are editions of I.E. which read: “If (*ve-im*) there be yet many years…and if (*ve-im*) there remain but few years.” The latter is also the reading in *Vat. Ebr.* 38. If the latter is correct then I.E.’s version of the Torah reads *ve-im* while our Torah in verse 51 reads *im*. It is possible that I.E. quoted from memory and erred. For the unaispirated *fa*, see I.E. on verse 34 and the notes thereto. It means¹⁷³ whether the remaining years be many or few.

footnotes:
¹⁷² In other words, the vav
¹⁷³ The meaning of verses 51 and 52.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:51:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:51:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:51:2)


**לפיהן ישיב גאלתו.** על כסף מקנתו כן יחשב לו:


ACCORDING UNTO THEM, HE SHALL GIVE BACK THE PRICE OF HIS REDEMPTION. It¹⁷⁴ shall be so considered with regard to the money that he was bought for.¹⁷⁵

footnotes:
¹⁷⁴ The remaining years.
¹⁷⁵ The remaining years until the Jubilee.



### Verse 53

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:53:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:53:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:53:1)


**כשכיר שנה.** להוסיף לא ירדנו בפרך לעיניך לא נעזוב הגר לרדות בו בפרך:


AS A SERVANT HIRED YEAR BY YEAR. This¹⁷⁶. is repeated¹⁷⁷ in order to add that which follows.¹⁷⁸. HE SHALL NOT RULE WITH RIGOR OVER HIM IN THY SIGHT. We shall not permit the stranger to rule over him with rigor.¹⁷⁹ means you will not permit him to rule with rigor over him. See I.E. on verse 43 and the notes thereto for the definition of rigor. Verse 43 prohibits an Israelite from ruling over an Israelite slave with rigor. Scripture now prohibits the Israelites from allowing strangers to rule over Israelites with rigor.

footnotes:
¹⁷⁶ As a servant hired year by year
¹⁷⁷ Scripture had already told us in verse 40 that the Hebrew slave is to be treated as a hired servant.
¹⁷⁸ That is, he shall not rule with rigor over him in thy sight
¹⁷⁹ He shall not rule with rigor over him



### Verse 54

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:54:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:54:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:54:1)


**ואם לא יגאל באלה.** בשנים הנשארות ליובל ויש אומרים על ידי אלה הנזכרים:


AND IF HE BE NOT REDEEMED BY ANY OF THESE MEANS.¹⁸⁰ (literally, in these or by these). The years remaining until the Jubilee.¹⁸¹ Others say by those mentioned earlier.¹⁸² is to be rendered in these. According to the second interpretation *ba-elleh* is to be rendered by these.

footnotes:
¹⁸⁰ Hebrew, be-elleh
¹⁸¹ This interpretation renders our clause, and if he be not redeemed in any of these, i.e., in any of these years.
¹⁸² This interpretation renders our clause, and if he be not redeemed by any of these, i.e., by any of the relatives mentioned in verses 48-49. According to the first interpretation be-elleh



### Verse 55

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 25:55:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:55:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:55:1)


**כי לי בני ישראל.** טעם שתודיעו כן הגר הקונה העבד הישראלי:


FOR UNTO ME THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL ARE SERVANTS. This means you shall inform the stranger who buys an Israelite as a servant that the children of Israel are My servants.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:1:1)


**לא תעשו לכם אלילם.** בעבור שנמכר לגוי הזהיר על עבודת כוכבים שלא ישמש אדוניו בפסל ולא יעבוד אדוניו בשבת ובזה יקנהו הנכרי:


YE SHALL MAKE YOU NO IDOLS. Scripture speaks of an Israelite who was sold to a non-Jew.¹ It therefore warns the Israelite against the worship of idols. He (the Israelite slave) must not serve his master in anything relating to an idol. The Israelite shall also not serve his master on the Sabbath.² The foreigner can purchase an Israelite only under these conditions.

footnotes:
¹ Lev. 25:47-55.
² Hence the Sabbath was mentioned in verse 2.



### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:1)


**ומקדשי תיראו.** שיבא למקדש שלש רגלים ויתכן להיות טעם לא תעשו לכם אלילים בעבור שאמר עבדי הם ואחר שהם עבדי יעבדו אותי לבדי ולא אחרים:


AND REVERENCE MY SANCTUARY. By coming to the sanctuary during the three pilgrimage festivals. It is possible that Scripture states *Ye shall make you no idols* because Scripture had earlier stated, *they are My servants* (Lev. 25:55). Now, since they are My servants they shall serve Me alone and not other gods.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:2)


**ופסל.** ידוע:


NEITHER…A GRAVEN IMAGE. The meaning of *pesel* (a graven image) is known.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:3)


**ומצבה.** לפסל:


OR A PILLAR. For a graven image.³

footnotes:
³ According to I.E. Scripture only prohibited a pillar made for the purpose of idol worship. See I.E. on Deut. 16:22 (Vol. 5, p. 117).


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:4)


**משכית.** שם כמו מרבית וטעמו אבן מצויירת מגזרת עברו משכיות לבב שכיות החמד׳‎:


FIGURED. *Maskit* (figured) is a noun. It is similar to *marbit* (increase) (Lev. 25:36).⁴ and have similar forms. The root of *maskit* is *sin, kaf, heh*. The root of *marbit* is *resh, bet, heh*. *Maskit* refers to a stone with figures on it. It is related to the word *maskiyyot* (imaginations) in *the imaginations of their heart* (Ps. 73)⁵ and *sekhiyyot* (imagery) in *delightful imagery* (Is. 2:16).

footnotes:
⁴ Both words are nouns that come from a root that end in a heh
⁵ That which their hearts see.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:5)


**לא תתנו בארצכם.** זה משפט עבודת כוכבים במרקוליס:


NEITHER SHALL YE PLACE…IN YOUR LAND. This⁶ is the way pagans worship Mercury.

footnotes:
⁶ Setting up a stone with figures on it.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:6)


**כי אני ה׳‎ אלהיכם.** לי תשתחוו. ובתרגום ירושלמי כן.


FOR I AM THE LORD YOUR GOD. You shall bow to Me. It⁷. is so explained in the Jerusalem Targum.⁸ by *setatyu chakik be-tziyurin* (a stone engraved with images).

footnotes:
⁷ The meaning of even maskit
⁸ The reference is to what is now referred to as Targum Jonathan. The latter renders even maskit


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:7)


**את שבתותי תשמרו.** שנות השמיטה:


YE SHALL KEEP MY SABBATHS. The Sabbatical years.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:8)


**ומקדשי תיראו.** שנת היובל. כי כן כתוב קדש תהיה לכם ולפי דעתי שהזכיר את שבתותי מטעם ומדי שבת בשבתו יבא כל בשר להשתחוות לפני ולא בעבור שהזכיר להשתחוות עליה והטעם שתבואו בכל שבת להשתחות לפני ולא על אבן משכית רק מקדשי והנה הזכיר היום הנבחר כי הוא נבחר לעבודת השם כאשר פירשתי וגם הזכיר המקום הנבחר:


AND REVERENCE MY SANCTUARY. The year of Jubilee, for Scripture clearly states, *it*⁹ *shall be holy unto you* (Lev. 25:12).I believe that Scripture employs *My sabbaths* in the sense of *And from one sabbath to another, Shall all flesh come to bow before Me*¹⁰ (Is. 66:23),¹¹ refers to the seventh day of the week, not to the Sabbatical year. for Scripture mentions *to bow down unto it*¹² 38 and Filwarg. *Mikra’ot Gedolot* reads, and not (*ve-lo*) because Scripture mentioned *to bow down unto it*. The reading in *Mikra’ot Gedolot* is very hard to explain and appears to be in error. See Filwarg. (v. 1).¹³ indicates that *My sabbaths* refers to the seventh day of the week, not to the Sabbatical year. For Is. 66:2 tells us that the Sabbath is the time that people are to come and bow before the Lord. The meaning of *Ye shall keep My sabbaths* is you shall come every Sabbath to bow before Me, and not to a stone with figures on it. You shall come to My sanctuary alone. Note, Scripture mentions the chosen day,¹⁴ for this day, as I have previously noted,¹⁵ was chosen for the worship of God. Scripture also mentions the chosen place.¹⁶

footnotes:
⁹ The year of Jubilee.
¹⁰ Translated literally.
¹¹ In other words, My sabbaths
¹² So Vat. Ebr.
¹³ To bow down unto it
¹⁴ The Sabbath.
¹⁵ See I.E. on Ex. 20:8 (Vol. 2, pp. 425,426).
¹⁶ God’s sanctuary.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:2:9)


וטעם **אני ה׳‎.** ששבתי מכל מלאכה בשבת וכבודי דר במקדש על כן הזכיר מלת תיראו:


The meaning of *for I am the Lord* is, for I rested from all work on the Sabbath and My glory dwells in the sanctuary. Scripture therefore says *and reverence*.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁷ My sanctuary.



### Verse 3

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:3:1)


**אם בחקתי. תשמרו ועשיתם.** מצוה ללמוד וללמד ולעשו׳‎:


IF YE WALK IN MY STATUTES, AND KEEP…AND DO. This is a commandment to study, to teach, and to observe.



### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:4:1)


וי״ו **ונתתי** כוי״ו והארץ היתה תהו ובהו ולעולם כאשר המלה מלרע עם וי״ו הטעם הוא לעתיד ואם מלעיל עבר חוץ ממתי מעט:


[THEN I WILL GIVE.] The *vav* in *ve-natati* (then I will give) is like the *vav* in *ve-ha’aretz* (now the earth) in *Now the earth was unformed and void* (Gen. 1:2).¹⁸ in *ve-natati* (then I will give), like the *vav* in *ve-ha’aretz* (now the earth), does not introduce an independent verse. According to I.E. the meaning of *ve-natati* is not “and I will give,” but “then I will give.” For the *vav* in *ve-ha’aretz*, see I.E. on Gen. 1:2 (Vol. 1, p. 30). When a word which has a *vav* prefixed to it¹⁹ (then 1 will give). is ultimately accented,²⁰ (then I will give). the word is an imperfect. When it is penultimately accented it is in the perfect. The above is, aside from a few instances, always the case.

footnotes:
¹⁸ In other words, the vav
¹⁹ Such as ve-natati
²⁰ As is the case with ve-natati


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:4:2)


**יבולה.** מן כי בול הרים ישאו לו ולא נדע אם היו״ד שרש או אינו שרש כמלת יקום:


HER PRODUCE. The word *yevulah* (her produce) is related to the word *vul* (food) in *Surely the mountains bring him forth food* (Job 40:20). We do not know whether the *yod* in *yevul* is a root letter or is like the *yod* in *yekum* (living substance) (Gen.7:23),²¹. which is not a root letter.

footnotes:
²¹ From the root kof, vav, mem



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:1)


**דיש את בציר.** והנה התבאר באספך את מעשיך מן השדה וכרם:


THRESHING SHALL REACH UNTO THE VINTAGE. Note, *when thou gatherest in thy labors out of the field* (Ex. 23:16) is now explained as meaning from the field and the vineyard.²²

footnotes:
²² Field in Ex. 23:16 refers to these two types of produce, for our verse speaks of threshing and vintage.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:2)


**לשבע.** שם הפועל:


YE HAVE ENOUGH. *Sova* (enough) is an infinitive.²³ is a variation of *li-sebo’a*.

footnotes:
²³ Even though it has the form of a verb. La-sova


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:3)


**לבטח.** בלמ״ד ובחסרונו שוה וכן מלת לבדד והנה הזכיר בתחלה השובע שהוא עיקר:


SAFELY. *La-vetach* (safely) has the same meaning whether spelled with a *lamed* or not.²⁴ (and dwell in…safely). I.E. says that the verse would have the same meaning if it read *vi-yeshavtem betach*. The same is true of the word *levadad* (alone) (Num. 23:9).²⁵ and *levad* have the same meaning. Note, Scripture mentions *sova* (enough) first,²⁶ for that is most important.

footnotes:
²⁴ Our verse reads vi-yeshavtem la-vetach
²⁵ Levadad
²⁶ The blessing of abundance of food is mentioned before other divine gifts.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:5:4)


וטעם **וישבתם לבטח.** כי בימי הרעב יגלו בני אדם ממקומם כטעם לא תוסף תת כחה לך נע ונד:


Scripture states, *and dwell in your land safely*, because people wander from their place in days of famine. Its meaning is similar to *it shall not henceforth yield unto thee her strength; a fugitive and a wanderer shalt thou be* (Gen. 4:12).



### Verse 6

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:6:1)


**ונתתי שלום בארץ.** ביניכם:


AND I WILL GIVE PEACE IN THE LAND. Among yourselves.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:6:2)


**ואין מחריד.** לא מחיה רעה ולא מאויב רק אתם תרדפו האויב ויפול לפניכם:


AND NONE SHALL MAKE YOU AFRAID. No evil beast and no enemy.²⁷ On the contrary, you will chase your enemies and they shall fall before you.

footnotes:
²⁷ Shall make you afraid.



### Verse 8

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:8:1)


והתמה כי מתי מעט מכם ירדפו מתי רב מהאויבים וכבר הודעתיך בספר מאזנים כי עשרה ומאה ואלף ורבבה שהיא עשרת אלפים סך חשבון ומנהג המרבה לומר אחד לעשר כמו כי עתה כמונו עשרת אלפים ופה הוסיף כי המאה ירדפום חמשה והנה כפל מנהג המרבה וכל אחד מהמאה ירדוף מאה ויש אומרים כי בעבור התחברות האחדים יוסיף המספר וכן איכה ירדוף אחד אלף. ואין צורך:


The greater wonder shall be that a small number of you shall pursue a large number of your enemies. I have previously informed you in my book *Moznayim* that ten, one hundred, a thousand, and ten thousand are round numbers.²⁸ It is customary for one who magnifies a number to say “one to ten.”²⁹ Compare, *but thou art worth ten thousand of us* (II Sam 18:3).³⁰ Scripture here mentions that a hundred shall be chased by five.³¹ Note, Scripture doubled the number used by the one who magnifies.³² Each one of a hundred Israelites shall chase a hundred of their enemies.³³. There are those who say that Scripture adds to the number because the number is increased when the individuals come together.³⁴ Similarly, *How should one chase a thousand* (Deut. 32:30).³⁵. Here too the Israelites accomplish more when they fight together. There is no need for this.³⁶

footnotes:
²⁸ Literally, are the sum of the count. The ones end in ten, the tens end in a hundred, the hundreds end in a thousand, and the thousands end in ten thousand. I.E.’s point is that if a person wants to exaggerate “ones,” he says ten; if he wants to exaggerate tens, he says a hundred; to exaggerate hundreds he says a thousand; if he wants to exaggerate thousands, he says ten thousand.
²⁹ If one wants to say that one person shall pursue many, one says he shall pursue ten. If one wants to say that a hundred shall pursue many, one says one hundred shall pursue a thousand, and so forth.
³⁰ Ten thousand is not to be taken literally.
³¹ The usual way of making the point that Scripture is making is to say that ten shall pursue a hundred.
³² If ten chase a hundred, then five chase fifty. However, Scripture tells us that five shall purse a hundred. Hence it doubles the amount that the Israelites would pursue.
³³ For Scripture states, and a hundred of you shall chase ten thousand
³⁴ Five Israelites shall chase a hundred of their enemies but a hundred Israelites shall chase ten thousand of their enemies because a hundred Israelites fighting together can achieve a greater victory per person than five Israelites fighting together.
³⁵ The verse continues, And two put ten thousand to flight
³⁶ For Scripture employs exaggerated numbers which are not to be taken literally.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:8:2)


**ונפלו אויביכם.** פעם אחרת שיפלו פעם אחר פעם בלי תקומה:


AND YOUR ENEMIES SHALL FALL. This is stated a second time.³⁷ The repetition indicates that they will fall time after time. They will not rise again.

footnotes:
³⁷ Scripture had already stated in the previous verse that Israel’s enemies would fall before them.



### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:9:1)


**ופניתי אליכם.** להרבות עשרכם ולהפרות ולהרבות אתכם בבנים ובבנות והנכון בעיני להיות והפריתי הפך עוצר רחם:


AND I WILL HAVE RESPECT UNTO YOU. To greatly increase your wealth, to make you fruitful, and to multiply you with sons and daughters. It appears to me that *and make you fruitful* is the reverse of *the barren womb* (Prov. 30:15).³⁸

footnotes:
³⁸ In this case our verse is to be understood as, I will ensure that you are not barren and I will multiply you.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:9:2)


**והקימתי את בריתי אתכם.** להיותכם ככוכבי השמים לרוב וכעפר הארץ:


AND WILL ESTABLISH MY COVENANT WITH YOU. In that you will be *as the stars of heaven for multitude* (Deut.1:11) and *as the dust of the earth* (Gen. 13:16).



### Verse 10

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:10:1)


**ואכלתם ישן נושן.** התמה שתהיו רבים ומרוב התבואה יאכל מי שירצה ישן גם נושן יותר מהישן והוא מבנין נפעל ויש מי שיוציא הישן מביתו מפני החדש כי אין לו מקום לשומו שם ומפרש אמר כי טעם **תוציאו.** אל השדה:


AND YE SHALL EAT OLD STORE LONG KEPT. Amazingly, even though you shall be numerous there shall be so much produce that whoever will want to, shall eat from the old store and also from the old store long kept, which is older than the old store. The word *noshan* (long kept) is a *nifal*. There will be those who shall remove the old store from their house to make room for the new produce, for they will have no place to store it.³⁹. There is a commentator who says that the meaning of *and ye shall bring forth* is you shall take it out to the field.⁴⁰

footnotes:
³⁹ I.E.’s interpretation of and ye shall bring forth the old from before the new
⁴⁰ To use as seed for the new crop.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:11:1)


**ונתתי משכני.** ולא תפחדו שתבואו לעולם לידי חסרון כי כבודי דר עמכם ואיננו כבן אדם שתגעל נפשו לשבת כלי במקום אחד והנה אני אהיה לכם לאלהים ואתם עמי כי על כן הוצאתי אתכם מארץ מצרים ועוד כי בלכתכם אל ארץ אויביכם והמקדש איננו עמכ׳‎ יתהלך כבודי בתוככם:


AND I WILL SET MY TABERNACLE. You will not fear that you might ever lack anything, for My glory shall dwell among you. I am not like a human being who abhors to dwell in one place.⁴¹ Furthermore, when you go into the land of your enemies⁴² and the temple is no longer among you,⁴³ My glory shall go with you. Note, I shall be your God. You shall be My people, for I took you out of the land of Egypt for that purpose.

footnotes:
⁴¹ I.E. might be hinting here as to why he chose a transient life.
⁴² When you go into exile.
⁴³ For it will be destroyed.



### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:13:1)


**מטת עולכם.** משל כפר החורש שיעבוד אדמת בעליו וישראל השתעבדו בבנין האדמה כי כן כתוב:


AND I HAVE BROKEN THE BARS OF YOUR YOKE. Scripture employs the metaphor of a plowing bullock⁴⁴ that works the land of its owner, for Israel was forced to work as slaves in improving the land. The latter is clearly stated.⁴⁵

footnotes:
⁴⁴ Israel is compared to a plowing bullock who has a yoke attached to its neck.
⁴⁵ Ex. 1:14.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:13:2)


**קוממיות.** בקומה זקופה:


UPRIGHT. *Kommemiyyut* means upright.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:14:1)


וריקי מוח אמרו כי הקללו׳‎ רבות מהברכות ולא אמרו אמת רק נאמרו הברכות כלל ונאמרו בקללות פרטים לירא ולהפחיד. השומעי׳‎ והמסתכל היטב יתברר לו דברי:


The empty heads say that there are more curses than blessings. However, they do not speak the truth. What Scripture does is to speak of the blessings in general terms. However, it lists the curses in detail in order to frighten and scare the listeners. What I say will be clear to the one who reads the text carefully.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:14:2)


**את כל המצות.** אלה הכתובים:


ALL THESE COMMANDMENTS. These that are written.⁴⁶

footnotes:
⁴⁶ The commandments written in the Torah.



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:15:1)


**להפרכם.** מלה זרה בדקדוק כי משקלי שמות הפעלים משתנים:


BUT BREAK. The word *le-hafrekhem* (but break) is grammatically irregular,⁴⁷ or *la-haferkhem* for the forms of verbs change.⁴⁸

footnotes:
⁴⁷ The usual form is la-hafirkhem
⁴⁸ That is, Scripture occasionally employs irregular forms.



### Verse 16

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:1)


**והפקדתי עליכם.** כאדם פקיד על אחר יעשה חפצו בו:


AND I WILL APPOINT…OVER YOU. As one who is appointed over another does whatever he wants, to him.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:2)


**בהלה את השחפת ואת הקדח׳‎.** חליים ידועים. וטעם **את.** עם:


TERROR…EVEN CONSUMPTION AND FEVER. Common diseases. The meaning of *et*⁴⁹ (even) is “with”.

footnotes:
⁴⁹ Here. See I.E. on Gen. 1:1 (Vol. 1, p. 27).


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:3)


וטעם **בהלה.** שתבהלו ולא תדעו מה תעשו וי״א כי בהל׳‎ כפתאום ורבים אמרו כי השחפת והקדחת שנים חליים בזרע כמו השדפון והירקון כי כן כתוב בעבור וזרעתם לריק ואין צורך כי פירושו שיבואו להם חליים ואם באו שוללים לכפרים יאכלו הזרע כי אין מי שיצא אליהם לגרשם:


The meaning of *behalah* (terror) is that you will be terrified and will not know what to do. There are those who say that *behalah* (terror) means suddenly.⁵⁰ Many say that *shachefet* (consumption) and *kadachat* (fever) are diseases of the seed like blasting⁵¹. and mildew,⁵². for Scripture clearly states that the latter are such diseases (Amos 4:9).⁵³ They say this⁵⁴ (consumption) and *kadachat* (fever) are diseases of the seed. because our verse states, *and ye shall sow your seed in vain*. However, there is no need for this,⁵⁵ (consumption) and *kadachat* (fever) as diseases of the seed on the basis of the verse’s stating *and ye shall sow your seed in vain*. for the meaning of our verse is that they shall be struck with illnesses⁵⁶ (consumption) and *kadachat* (fever). and if marauders shall come to the villages they shall then eat their seed, for there will not be anyone to go out and scatter the raiders.⁵⁷.

footnotes:
⁵⁰ The diseases will strike suddenly and you will not be able to guard against them.
⁵¹ Hebrew, shiddafon
⁵² Hebrew, yerakon
⁵³ We know that blasting and mildew are diseases of the seed because Scripture speaks thus of them in Amos 4:9.
⁵⁴ That shachefet
⁵⁵ To interpret shachefet
⁵⁶ That is, with shachefet
⁵⁷ I.E.’s interpretation of and ye shall sow your seed in vain, for your enemies shall eat it


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:16:4)


וטעם **מכלות עינים.** שאלה החליים יחשכו עינים וידאבו הנפש והאל״ף חסר ממלת ומדיבות נפש וכן ממלת שקר מזין וי״א כי העינים מן הגוף בעבור ומדיבו׳‎ נפש ואין צורך:


The meaning of *that shall make the eyes to fail* is that these diseases will darken the eyes and languish the soul. The *alef* is missing in the word *medivot* (languish) in and *the soul to languish*. It is similarly missing in the word *mezin* (giveth ear) in *And a liar giveth ear* (Prov.17:4). Some say that *the eyes to fail* speaks of the eyes of the body⁵⁸ is to be taken literally, not as a metaphor. because it goes on to say *and the soul to languish*.⁵⁹ However, there is no need for this interpretation.⁶⁰ refers to spiritual pain so does *the eyes to fail*.

footnotes:
⁵⁸ In other words, the eyes to fail
⁵⁹ The soul will languish because the eyes fail.
⁶⁰ For just as and the soul to languish



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:17:1)


**ונתתי פני.** קצפי ואפי כמו ופניה לא היו לה והטעם כי הנשארים שאין להם חליים אם יצאו אל האויבים ינגפו לפניהם ופעם ינוסו ואין רודף כטעם מחנה ארם:


AND I WILL SET MY FACE AGAINST YOU. My wrath and My anger.⁶¹ means My anger. Compare, *and her countenance*⁶² *was no more* (I Sam. 1:18).⁶³ The meaning of our verse is that those that remain and are not stricken by disease will be smitten before their enemies, should they go out to fight them. At other times they will flee when no one pursues them as in the case of the Aramean camp.⁶⁴

footnotes:
⁶¹ My face
⁶² Or her face.
⁶³ According to I.E. this is to be interpreted as she no longer was angry.
⁶⁴ See II Kings 7:6,7. The Arameans panicked and fled because they thought that they were about to be attacked by a superior force. In reality, no one was about to engage them.



### Verse 18

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:18:1)


**ואם עד אלה.** המכות:


AND IF YE WILL NOT YET FOR THESE THINGS. The plagues.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:18:2)


**שבע.** בעבור היותו חשבון שלם נאמר על לשון רבים וכן כי שבע יפול צדיק וקם עד עקרה ילדה שבעה ואין על חטאתיכם להוסיף שבעה רק ויספתי פעם אחרת כמו קול גדול ולא יסף:


SEVEN. The number seven is used for “many” because it is a complete number.⁶⁵ Similarly, *For a righteous man falleth seven times* (Prov. 24:16); *While the barren hath born seven* (I Sam. 2:5). *Al chatatekhem* (for your sins) does not add the number seven to each sin.⁶⁶ does not mean that they will be punished seven times for each sin. Its meaning is, I will punish you again for each sin. *Ve-yasafti* (then …more) is similar to *yasaf* (more) in *and it went on no more* (Deut. 5:19).

footnotes:
⁶⁵ It has special significance. See I.E. on Num. 23:1.
⁶⁶ Then I will chastise you seven times more for your sins


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:18:3)


וטעם **על חטאתיכם.** בעבור חטאתיכם וי״א שהחשבון כמשמעו והראשון נכון כי אם נחשוב ושברתי אחד שמיכם כברזל שנים וארץ כנחושה ותם לריק ויבול הארץ ופרי העץ הנה שש ומכת חית השדה הנה שבע:


The meaning of *al chatatekhem*⁶⁷ literally means on your sins. Hence I.E.’s comments. is, because of your sins. Some say that the number seven is to be taken literally.⁶⁸. However, the first interpretation⁶⁹ is correct, for if we count *And I will break…*(v. 19) as one; *your heaven as iron* (v. 19) as two; *and your earth as brass* (v. 19);⁷⁰ *And your strength shall be spent in vain*⁷¹ (v. 20); *for your land…her produce*⁷² (v. 20); *the trees of the land…their fruit* (v. 20), then we have six. Note, the plague of *beast of the field* (v. 22) makes seven.⁷³ after it states, I will bring seven times more plagues upon you. If Scripture was listing the seven plagues that God would bring upon Israel, then the plague of the beasts would have been mentioned before the statement, I will bring seven times more plagues upon you.

footnotes:
⁶⁷ Al chatatekhem
⁶⁸ This is the opinion of Rashi, Targum Jonathan, and the Sifra
⁶⁹ That the number seven is not to be taken literally.
⁷⁰ As three.
⁷¹ As four.
⁷² As five.
⁷³ Scripture mentions the plague of beast of the field



### Verse 19

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:19:1)


**גאון עוזכם** השובע כטעם וישמן ישורון והגאה אם ישבר יפול וישח:


THE PRIDE OF YOUR POWER. The satiated,⁷⁴ as in *But Jeshurun waxed fat, and kicked* (Deut. 32:15). When the proud are broken they fall and are brought low.

footnotes:
⁷⁴ From an abundance of food.



### Verse 20

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:20:1)


**ותם לריק כחכם.** שיגעתם בעבודת האדמה:


AND YOUR STRENGTH SHALL BE SPENT IN VAIN. The strength which you expended in working the soil.



### Verse 21

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:21:1)


**קרי.** אמרו רבים שפירושו גבורה ונצוח כאיש שיחזק לבו ולא יפחד למען שלא ינוצח ואין ריע לו וי״א שהוא מגזרת כל הקורו׳‎ והטעם שאמר בדברי הפלשתים כי לא ידו נגעה בנו מקרה הוא ועל ב׳‎ הפירושי׳‎ יחסר בי״ת כמו כי ששת ימים או יהי כן תלכו עמי הליכת קרי כאשר הראיתיך רבים כאלה והנה כתוב כחטאתיכם ולא יוסיף עליכם ועל חטאתיכם דבק עם ליסרה אתכם הטעם בעבור חטאתיכם:


CONTRARY. Many say that the meaning of *keri* (contrary) is power and might. You will act like a person who hardens his heart and is not afraid that he will be defeated.⁷⁵ The word *keri* has no neighbor.⁷⁶ Other say that the word *keri* is related to *korot* (befallen) in *all that had befallen* (Gen. 42:29). Its meaning is found in what Scripture says in the account of the Philistines wherein we read, *it is not His hand that smote us; it was a chance* (mikreh) *that happened to us*⁷⁷ (I Sam. 6:9). According to both interpretations the word *keri* is missing a *bet*.⁷⁸ should be read as if written *be-keri*. According to I.E. our verse literally reads, And if you walk hardened with Me, or And if you walk in chance with Me. Hence I.E.’s comment. It is like the word *sheshet*⁷⁹. (six) in *for in six days* (Ex. 20:11).⁸⁰ Or, *ve-im telekhu immi keri* (and if you walk contrary unto Me) is to be read as if written *ve-im telekhu immi halikhat keri* (if you walk, a contrary walk, unto Me).⁸¹ is to be understood as the walk of a person with a hardened heart, or the walk of a person who does not believe in divine providence. In this case the word *halikhat* is missing from our text. I have already shown you many such instances.⁸² Note, our verse reads *according to your sins*. God will not add to the sins.⁸³ (v. 18; v. 24) as in addition to or more than your sins. *Al chatatekhem* (for your sins) (v. 18) is connected to *le-yasserah etkhem* (chastise you). The meaning of *al chatatekhem* (for your sins)⁸⁴ is because of your sins.

footnotes:
⁷⁵ According to this interpretation our verse is to be understood as follows: And if you behave (walk) as if that which happens occurs by chance. In other words, if you act as if that which happens is not the result of Divine Providence.
⁷⁶ It is not found again in Scripture.
⁷⁷ According to this interpretation our verse reads, And if you walk in chance with Me, i.e., believing that all that which happens is by chance.
⁷⁸ In other words, keri
⁷⁹ Which is to be read as if written be-sheshet
⁸⁰ Literally, for six days. Hence I.E.’s comments.
⁸¹ According to this interpretation halikhat keri
⁸² Where Scripture omits a word.
⁸³ I.E. notes this so that we do not render al chatatekhem
⁸⁴ Not in addition to or more than your sins.



### Verse 22

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:22:1)


**והשלחתי בכם.** מגזר׳‎ הנני משליח בך:


AND I WILL SEND…AMONG YOU. *Ve-hishlakhti* (and I will send) is related to the word *mashli’ach* (send) in *behold, I will send* (Ex.8: 17).⁸⁵

footnotes:
⁸⁵ In other words, “send” here has the meaning of sending misfortune.

WHICH SHALL ROB YOU OF YOUR CHILDREN. By killing your little ones.

AND MAKE YOU FEW IN NUMBER. By the death of adults.

**ונשמו דרכיכם.** כי אין דרך בטוח מפחד החיות שאינם ברשות אדם כאשר פירשתי:


AND YOUR WAYS SHALL BECOME DESOLATE. For there will be no way that will be secure from the fear of wild animals.⁸⁶ I have previously explained this.⁸⁷

### Verse 23

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:23:1)

### Verse 24

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:24:1)

FOR YOUR SINS. *Al chatatekhem* (for your sins) is connected to *ve-hikketi* (and I will smite).⁸⁹

### Verse 25

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:25:1)


**נקם ברית.** על כן אמרתי כי זאת הברית הכרותה בסיני הכתובה בפרשת אלה המשפטי׳‎ יום קבלו על נפשם ואמרו נעשה ונשמע אז נכרתה הברי׳‎ ואמר. להם כל הפרשה הזאת:


THE VENGEANCE OF THE COVENANT. Our verse mentions the covenant.⁹⁰ On the basis of this I earlier said⁹¹ that our Torah portion contains the covenant referred to in the Torah reading *Now these are the ordinances* (Ex. 21:1). The covenant was made on the day Israel promised [*All that the Lord hath spoken* ] *will we do, and obey* (Ex. 24:7). The covenant was then made and Moses told them this portion.⁹²

footnotes:
⁹⁰ The only covenant that God made with Israel up to this point was the covenant at Sinai. Hence I.E.’s comment.
⁹¹ See I.E. on Lev. 25:1.
⁹² The blessing and the curses contained in this Torah portion.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:25:2)


**ונאספתם אל עריכם.** מפני החרב ושם אשלח הדבר והרעב עד שתרצו להנתן ביד האויב וזה טעם **ונתתם ביד אויב** והיא מלה מבנין נפעל:


AND YE SHALL BE GATHERED TOGETHER WITHIN YOUR CITIES. Because of the sword.⁹³. There I will send the pestilence and the famine. The pestilence and the famine will cause you to desire to be delivered into the hand of the enemy. This is the meaning of *and ye shall be delivered into the hand of the enemy. Ve-nittattem* (and ye shall be delivered) is a *nifal*.

footnotes:
⁹³ Mentioned earlier in the verse, a sword…that shall execute the vengeance of the covenant



### Verse 26

###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26:1)


**בשברי.** שם הפעל ובא שבור כמו בשכבה ובקומה וטעם השבר בעבור מטה לחם והוא דרך משל ובישעיה משען לחם כי הלחם לבב אנוש יסעד:


WHEN I BREAK. *Shivri* (I break) is an infinitive. It is vocalized with a *chirik*.⁹⁴. Compare, *shikhvah* (she lay down) in *when she lay down, nor when she arose* (Gen. 19:33).⁹⁵ rather than with a small *kamatz*. The Torah employs the term *break* because it speaks of the *staff of bread*.⁹⁶ The latter is a metaphor. We find that Isaiah employs the phrase *stay*⁹⁷ (a staff). *of bread* (Is.3:1), for bread *stays man’s heart* (Ps. 104:15).

footnotes:
⁹⁴ Rather than with a small kamatz
⁹⁵ It too is an infinitive and is vocalized with a chirik
⁹⁶ A staff breaks.
⁹⁷ Hebrew, mishan


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26:2)


**עשר נשים.** כי הוא סך חשבון כי מנהג ישראל היה לאפות כל בית תנור לעצמו שיאכלו כל השבוע ומערכ׳‎ השבת תוכיח:


TEN WOMEN. Scripture says *ten women*, for ten is the last of the numbers.⁹⁸. It is the last of the single numbers. See I.E. on verse 6. It was the custom of Israel for each family to bake⁹⁹ one oven full of bread for them to eat for the entire week.¹⁰⁰ The arrangement of the Sabbath bread¹⁰¹. is proof of this.¹⁰²

footnotes:
⁹⁸ Hebrew, sakh ha-cheshbon
⁹⁹ On one day.
¹⁰⁰ According to I.E. the meaning of our verse is that there will be so little food that a lot of women will bake their week’s supply of bread in one oven.
¹⁰¹ The show bread, the lecham ha-panim
¹⁰² A week’s supply of show bread was baked every Friday (Sarim).

**והשיבו לחמכם במשקל.** בעבור שהיא מעט:


AND THEY SHALL DELIVER YOUR BREAD AGAIN BY WEIGHT. For it will consist of a small amount.¹⁰³

¹⁰³ There will be so little food that each morsel will be precious.


###### Ibn Ezra on Leviticus 26:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26:4)


**ואכלתם ולא תשבעו.** יש רעב וישבע האוכל ממעט ואתם מאוכל הרבה לא ישבעו:


AND YE SHALL EAT, AND NOT BE SATISFIED. There are hungry people who are satisfied by eating a little amount of food. However, you will not be satisfied from eating much food.¹⁰⁴

¹⁰⁴ See Rashi, “This is a curse within the intestines upon (which will affect) the bread.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:27:1)
